import type { LatticeHandoff } from "../contracts/handoff";
import type { EngineeringEntity,EngineeringRelationship,InvestigationGraph } from "../domain/model";
type Attr=Record<string,string|number|boolean>;
const qty=(a:Attr)=>{const v=a.Qty??a.Quantity;return typeof v==="string"||typeof v==="number"?v:undefined};
const normalize=(v:unknown)=>String(v??"").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,"");
const cleanSubject=(value:string)=>{const v=value.trim();if(!v)return"Engineering investigation";return v.replace(/_/g," ").replace(/^./,c=>c.toUpperCase())};
export function buildInvestigation(handoff:LatticeHandoff):InvestigationGraph{
 const entities:EngineeringEntity[]=[],relationships:EngineeringRelationship[]=[],sourceRoots:string[]=[],matches=new Map<string,{id:string;source:string;verified:boolean}[]>();
 handoff.sources.forEach((source,sourceIndex)=>{const visit=(node:typeof source.root,level:number,parent?:string,path:number[]=[sourceIndex])=>{const id=`${source.source}:${path.join(".")}:${node.id}`,attributes=node.attributes??{},entity:EngineeringEntity={id,sourceNodeId:node.id,source:source.source,name:node.name,kind:node.children?.length?"assembly":"component",level:level+1,attributes,provenance:{sourceLabel:source.label,nativeId:source.nativeId,capturedAt:source.capturedAt}};entities.push(entity);if(!parent)sourceRoots.push(id);else relationships.push({id:`contains:${parent}:${id}`,from:parent,to:id,kind:"contains",quantity:qty(attributes),label:qty(attributes)?`Contains Â· Qty ${qty(attributes)}`:"Contains",evidence:[{sourceLabel:source.label,reason:"Direct BOM parent-child occurrence",nativeId:source.nativeId,capturedAt:source.capturedAt}]});const native=String(attributes["Item ID"]??attributes["Part Number"]??attributes.Number??"");const key=normalize(native||node.name);if(key)matches.set(key,[...(matches.get(key)??[]),{id,source:source.source,verified:Boolean(native)}]);node.children?.forEach((child,index)=>visit(child,level+1,id,[...path,index]))};visit(source.root,0)});
 const subjectId=`subject:${normalize(handoff.subjectLabel)||handoff.handoffId}`,sources=handoff.sources.map(item=>item.source).filter((item):item is Exclude<typeof item,"excel">=>item!=="excel");
 entities.push({id:subjectId,sourceNodeId:handoff.subjectLabel,source:"unified",name:cleanSubject(handoff.subjectLabel),kind:"identity",level:0,attributes:{"Selected representations":sourceRoots.length,"Investigation query":handoff.subjectLabel},provenance:{sourceLabel:"Investigation subject",capturedAt:handoff.createdAt},representationSources:sources});
 sourceRoots.forEach(rootId=>{const root=entities.find(item=>item.id===rootId)!;const idEvidence=normalize(root.provenance.nativeId),query=normalize(handoff.subjectLabel),name=normalize(root.name),verified=Boolean(query&&(idEvidence.includes(query)||name.includes(query)||query.includes(name)));relationships.push({id:`represented:${subjectId}:${rootId}`,from:subjectId,to:rootId,kind:"represented-by",confidence:verified?1:.72,verified,label:verified?`Represented in ${root.provenance.sourceLabel} Â· Verified`:`Selected from ${root.provenance.sourceLabel} Â· Review`,evidence:[{sourceLabel:"Investigation selection",reason:verified?"Search subject agrees with the source root":"Selected by the user; identity review remains available"}]})});
 for(const group of matches.values()){const per=[...new Map(group.map(x=>[x.source,x])).values()],first=per[0];if(!first)continue;for(let i=1;i<per.length;i++){const other=per[i];if(!other)continue;const verified=first.verified&&other.verified;relationships.push({id:`corresponds:${first.id}:${other.id}`,from:first.id,to:other.id,kind:"corresponds-to",confidence:verified?1:.72,verified,label:verified?"Same part Â· Verified":"Likely same part Â· 72%",evidence:[{sourceLabel:"Cross-source correspondence",reason:verified?"Verified identifier match":"Normalized-name match"}]})}}
 const roots=[subjectId];return{entities,relationships,roots,byId:Object.fromEntries(entities.map(e=>[e.id,e])),relationshipById:Object.fromEntries(relationships.map(r=>[r.id,r]))};
}

===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\infrastructure\elk-layout-adapter.ts =====
import type {
  LayoutOrientation,
  NodePosition,
  RelationshipProjection,
} from "../contracts/projection";
import type { LayoutViewport } from "../layout/layout-contract";
import type { LayoutSession } from "../layout/layout-session";
import {
  classifyChange,
  createSession,
  movementDiagnostics,
  stabilizeLayout,
} from "../layout/layout-session";
import { routeRelationships } from "../layout/relationship-router";
import { applyRadialFirstHopRoutes } from "../layout/radial-route-adapter";
import { hybridLayout, NODE_HEIGHT, NODE_WIDTH } from "./hybrid-layout-planner";
export { NODE_HEIGHT, NODE_WIDTH };
export async function layoutProjection(
  projection: RelationshipProjection,
  _orientation: LayoutOrientation,
  signal: number,
  pinned: Readonly<Record<string, NodePosition>> = {},
  viewport?: LayoutViewport,
  previous?: LayoutSession,
) {
  const started = Date.now();
  const changeType = classifyChange(previous, projection, pinned, false);
  let result = await hybridLayout(projection, pinned, signal, viewport);
  result = stabilizeLayout(result, previous, changeType);
  const routed = routeRelationships(
    projection,
    result.positions,
    result.dimensions,
    result.branches,
    result.routes,
    pinned,
  );
  result = {
    ...result,
    routes: applyRadialFirstHopRoutes(routed.routes, result.radialConnections),
    diagnostics: {
      ...result.diagnostics,
      invalidRouteCount: routed.diagnostics.invalidRouteCount,
      routing: routed.diagnostics,
    },
  };
  const stability = movementDiagnostics(
    previous,
    result,
    changeType,
    Date.now() - started,
  );
  result = { ...result, diagnostics: { ...result.diagnostics, stability } };
  return { ...result, session: createSession(result, projection, signal) };
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\infrastructure\hybrid-layout-planner.ts =====
import ELK from "elkjs/lib/elk.bundled.js";
import type {
  NodePosition,
  RelationshipProjection,
} from "../contracts/projection";
import { decomposeProjection } from "../layout/branch-decomposition";
import { buildElkBranchGraph, nodeSize } from "../layout/elk-branch-builder";
import {
  adaptElkResult,
  type ElkGraphResult,
} from "../layout/elk-result-adapter";
import {
  LATTICE_LAYOUT_REVISION,
  type Bounds,
  type LayoutPort,
  type LayoutRoute,
  type LayoutViewport,
  type UniversalLayoutResult,
} from "../layout/layout-contract";
import {
  cardinalDirection,
  collisionFreeRadius,
  outwardAnchor,
  sectorAngles,
  usableViewport,
} from "../layout/sector-orchestrator";
import { radialConnectionGeometry } from "../layout/radial-connection-geometry";
import { anchorPinnedSubtrees } from "../layout/incremental-expansion-layout";
import {
  countBranchOverlaps,
  countInvalidRoutes,
  countNodeOverlaps,
  subjectIntrusions,
} from "../layout/layout-diagnostics";

export const NODE_WIDTH = 272;
export const NODE_HEIGHT = 96;
export const IDENTITY_WIDTH = 344;
export const IDENTITY_HEIGHT = 132;
const elk = new ELK();
const branchLayoutCache = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof adaptElkResult>>();
const MAX_BRANCH_CACHE = 160;
const branchCacheKey = (
  branch: {
    id: string;
    nodeIds: string[];
    edges: Array<{ id: string; source: string; target: string }>;
  },
  direction: string,
) =>
  `${direction}|${branch.id}|${branch.nodeIds.join(",")}|${branch.edges.map((edge) => `${edge.id}:${edge.source}>${edge.target}`).join(",")}`;
const now = () =>
  typeof performance === "undefined" ? Date.now() : performance.now();

export function radialChildPositions(
  center: NodePosition,
  childIds: readonly string[],
  radius: number,
  startAngle = -Math.PI / 2,
): Record<string, NodePosition> {
  const ordered = [...childIds].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b));
  const step = ordered.length ? (Math.PI * 2) / ordered.length : 0;
  return Object.fromEntries(
    ordered.map((id, index) => [
      id,
      {
        x: center.x + Math.cos(startAngle + index * step) * radius,
        y: center.y + Math.sin(startAngle + index * step) * radius,
      },
    ]),
  );
}
const shiftPoint = (
  point: NodePosition,
  offset: NodePosition,
): NodePosition => ({ x: point.x + offset.x, y: point.y + offset.y });
const fallbackBranch = (
  nodeIds: readonly string[],
  direction: ReturnType<typeof cardinalDirection>,
) => {
  const horizontal = direction === "RIGHT" || direction === "LEFT";
  const sign = direction === "LEFT" || direction === "UP" ? -1 : 1;
  const positions = Object.fromEntries(
    nodeIds.map((id, index) => [
      id,
      horizontal
        ? {
            x: sign * index * 376,
            y: (index % 2 ? 1 : -1) * Math.ceil(index / 2) * 144,
          }
        : {
            x: (index % 2 ? 1 : -1) * Math.ceil(index / 2) * 328,
            y: sign * index * 200,
          },
    ]),
  );
  const bounds = Object.values(positions).reduce<Bounds>(
    (b, p) => ({
      x: Math.min(b.x, p.x),
      y: Math.min(b.y, p.y),
      width: Math.max(b.x + b.width, p.x + NODE_WIDTH) - Math.min(b.x, p.x),
      height: Math.max(b.y + b.height, p.y + NODE_HEIGHT) - Math.min(b.y, p.y),
    }),
    { x: 0, y: 0, width: NODE_WIDTH, height: NODE_HEIGHT },
  );
  return {
    positions,
    dimensions: Object.fromEntries(
      nodeIds.map((id) => [id, { width: NODE_WIDTH, height: NODE_HEIGHT }]),
    ),
    ports: {},
    routes: {},
    bounds,
  };
};

export async function hybridLayout(
  projection: RelationshipProjection,
  pinned: Readonly<Record<string, NodePosition>> = {},
  signal = 0,
  viewport?: LayoutViewport,
): Promise<UniversalLayoutResult> {
  const started = now();
  const decomposition = decomposeProjection(projection);
  const baseDiagnostics = {
    strategy: "sector-layered-v2" as const,
    sourceCount: decomposition?.branches.length ?? 0,
    rings: decomposition?.branches.length ? 1 : 0,
    iterations: 0,
    collisionsResolved: 0,
    elapsedMs: 0,
    overlapCount: 0,
    invalidCoordinateCount: 0,
    fallbackBranches: 0,
    nodeOverlapCount: 0,
    branchOverlapCount: 0,
    subjectIntrusionCount: 0,
    invalidRouteCount: 0,
  };
  if (!decomposition)
    return {
      signal,
      revision: LATTICE_LAYOUT_REVISION,
      positions: {},
      dimensions: {},
      ports: {},
      routes: {},
      branchBounds: {},
      radialConnections: {},
      branches: [],
      diagnostics: baseDiagnostics,
    };

  const usable = usableViewport(viewport);
  const subjectNode = projection.nodes.find(
    (node) => node.id === decomposition.subjectId,
  )!;
  const subjectSize = nodeSize(subjectNode.kind);
  const positions: Record<string, NodePosition> = {
    [decomposition.subjectId]: {
      x: usable.center.x - subjectSize.width / 2,
      y: usable.center.y - subjectSize.height / 2,
    },
  };
  const dimensions = {
    [decomposition.subjectId]: subjectSize,
  } as UniversalLayoutResult["dimensions"];
  const ports: UniversalLayoutResult["ports"] = {},
    routes: UniversalLayoutResult["routes"] = {},
    branchBounds: UniversalLayoutResult["branchBounds"] = {};
  const subjectExclusionPadding = 80;

  const subjectExclusionBounds: Bounds = {
    x: positions[decomposition.subjectId].x - subjectExclusionPadding,
    y: positions[decomposition.subjectId].y - subjectExclusionPadding,
    width: subjectSize.width + subjectExclusionPadding * 2,
    height: subjectSize.height + subjectExclusionPadding * 2,
  };

  const branches: UniversalLayoutResult["branches"] = [];
  const accepted: Bounds[] = [subjectExclusionBounds];
  const angles = sectorAngles(
    decomposition.branches.map((branch) => branch.id),
  );
  let collisionsResolved = 0,
    fallbackBranches = 0;

  for (const sector of angles) {
    const branch = decomposition.branches.find(
      (candidate) => candidate.id === sector.id,
    )!;
    const direction = cardinalDirection(sector.angle);
    let local: ReturnType<typeof adaptElkResult>;
    const cacheKey = branchCacheKey(branch, direction);
    const cached = branchLayoutCache.get(cacheKey);
    if (cached) local = structuredClone(cached);
    else {
      try {
        const graph = buildElkBranchGraph(branch, projection, direction);
        local = adaptElkResult(
          (await elk.layout(graph)) as ElkGraphResult,
          branch.rootId,
          direction,
        );
      } catch {
        local = fallbackBranch(branch.nodeIds, direction);
        fallbackBranches++;
      }
      branchLayoutCache.set(cacheKey, structuredClone(local));
      if (branchLayoutCache.size > MAX_BRANCH_CACHE)
        branchLayoutCache.delete(branchLayoutCache.keys().next().value!);
    }
    const extent = Math.max(local.bounds.width, local.bounds.height);
    const baseRadius = Math.max(
      420,
      subjectSize.width / 2 + 160,
      Math.min(760, 300 + extent * 0.22),
    );
    const resolved = collisionFreeRadius(
      baseRadius,
      sector.angle,
      local.bounds,
      accepted,
      usable.center,
    );
    collisionsResolved += resolved.iterations;
    const anchor = outwardAnchor(usable.center, sector.angle, resolved.radius);
    for (const [id, p] of Object.entries(local.positions))
      positions[id] = shiftPoint(p, anchor);
    Object.assign(dimensions, local.dimensions);
    for (const [id, port] of Object.entries(local.ports) as [
      string,
      LayoutPort,
    ][])
      ports[id] = { ...port, position: shiftPoint(port.position, anchor) };
    for (const [id, route] of Object.entries(local.routes) as [
      string,
      LayoutRoute,
    ][])
      routes[id] = {
        ...route,
        start: shiftPoint(route.start, anchor),
        bends: route.bends.map((p: NodePosition) => shiftPoint(p, anchor)),
        end: shiftPoint(route.end, anchor),
      };
    const globalBounds = {
      x: anchor.x + local.bounds.x,
      y: anchor.y + local.bounds.y,
      width: local.bounds.width,
      height: local.bounds.height,
    };
    accepted.push(globalBounds);
    branchBounds[branch.id] = globalBounds;
    branches.push({
      id: branch.id,
      rootId: branch.rootId,
      nodeIds: [...branch.nodeIds],
      edgeIds: branch.edges.map((edge) => edge.id),
      direction,
      angle: sector.angle,
      bounds: globalBounds,
    });
  }

  const orphanAngles = sectorAngles(decomposition.orphanNodeIds, 0);
  for (const orphan of orphanAngles) {
    const id = orphan.id;
    const size = nodeSize(
      projection.nodes.find((node) => node.id === id)?.kind ?? "component",
    );
    const local = { x: -size.width / 2, y: -size.height / 2, ...size };
    const resolved = collisionFreeRadius(
      Math.max(760, 520 + accepted.length * 40),
      orphan.angle,
      local,
      accepted,
      usable.center,
    );
    const anchor = outwardAnchor(usable.center, orphan.angle, resolved.radius);
    positions[id] = {
      x: anchor.x - size.width / 2,
      y: anchor.y - size.height / 2,
    };
    dimensions[id] = size;
    accepted.push({ ...positions[id], ...size });
    collisionsResolved += resolved.iterations;
  }
  Object.assign(positions, anchorPinnedSubtrees(projection, positions, dimensions, pinned));

  const radialConnections: UniversalLayoutResult["radialConnections"] = {};
  for (const branch of branches) {
    const edge = projection.edges.find((candidate) =>
      candidate.source === decomposition.subjectId && candidate.target === branch.rootId,
    );
    const targetPosition = positions[branch.rootId];
    const targetSize = dimensions[branch.rootId];
    if (!edge || !targetPosition || !targetSize) continue;
    const geometry = radialConnectionGeometry({
      branchId: branch.id,
      angle: branch.angle,
      sourceCenter: positions[decomposition.subjectId],
      sourceSize: subjectSize,
      targetCenter: { x: targetPosition.x + targetSize.width / 2, y: targetPosition.y + targetSize.height / 2 },
      targetSize,
    });
    const source = { ...geometry.source, nodeId: decomposition.subjectId };
    const target = { ...geometry.target, nodeId: branch.rootId };
    ports[source.id] = source;
    ports[target.id] = target;
    radialConnections[edge.id] = {
      branchId: branch.id,
      angle: branch.angle,
      sourceNodeId: decomposition.subjectId,
      targetNodeId: branch.rootId,
      edgeId: edge.id,
      source,
      target,
    };
  }
  const invalidCoordinateCount = Object.values(positions).filter(
    (p) => !Number.isFinite(p.x) || !Number.isFinite(p.y),
  ).length;
  const subjectBounds = {
    ...positions[decomposition.subjectId],
    ...subjectSize,
  };
  const branchOverlapCount = countBranchOverlaps(Object.values(branchBounds));
  const nodeOverlapCount = countNodeOverlaps(positions, dimensions);
  const subjectIntrusionCount = subjectIntrusions(
    subjectBounds,
    Object.values(branchBounds),
  );
  const invalidRouteCount = countInvalidRoutes(routes);
  return {
    signal,
    revision: LATTICE_LAYOUT_REVISION,
    positions,
    dimensions,
    ports,
    routes,
    branchBounds,
    branches,
    radialConnections,
    diagnostics: {
      ...baseDiagnostics,
      iterations: branches.length,
      collisionsResolved,
      overlapCount: branchOverlapCount,
      invalidCoordinateCount,
      fallbackBranches,
      nodeOverlapCount,
      branchOverlapCount,
      subjectIntrusionCount,
      invalidRouteCount,
      elapsedMs: Math.round(now() - started),
    },
  };
}

export function mergeSourceIntelligence(parts:EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1[]):EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1{if(!parts.length)throw new Error("No source intelligence was returned.");if(parts.length===1)return parts[0];const base=parts[0],dedupe=<T extends{id:string}>(items:T[])=>[...new Map(items.map(x=>[x.id,x])).values()];const secondarySubjects:IntellicenceEntityFix[]=parts.slice(1).filter(x=>x.subject.id!==base.subject.id).map(x=>({id:x.subject.id,kind:"source-representation",displayName:x.subject.displayName,identityQuality:"native",assertion:"fact",properties:{preferredProductId:{type:"text",text:x.subject.preferredProductId??""},sourceRole:{type:"text",text:"participating-subject"}},provenance:x.subject.provenance,temporal:x.subject.temporal}));const entities=dedupe([...secondarySubjects,...parts.flatMap(x=>x.entities)]),relationships=dedupe(parts.flatMap(x=>x.relationships)),evidence=dedupe(parts.flatMap(x=>x.evidence)),findings=dedupe(parts.flatMap(x=>x.findings)),sources=dedupe(parts.flatMap(x=>x.sources));return{...base,investigationId:`federated-${parts.map(x=>x.investigationId).join("-")}`,subject:{...base.subject,candidateIds:[...new Set(parts.flatMap(x=>x.subject.candidateIds))],reasons:[...new Set(parts.flatMap(x=>x.subject.reasons))]},entities,relationships,evidence,findings,sources,identityClusters:dedupe(parts.flatMap(x=>x.identityClusters)),temporal:{...base.temporal,assembledAt:new Date().toISOString()},summary:{entityCount:entities.length,relationshipCount:relationships.length,evidenceCount:evidence.length,findingCount:findings.length,availableSourceCount:sources.filter(x=>x.status==="available").length,partialSourceCount:sources.filter(x=>x.status!=="available").length,status:sources.every(x=>x.status==="available")?"success":"partial-success"}}}

import { projectedNodeDimensions } from "../motion/node-sizing";
import type { LayoutOrientation, RelationshipProjection } from "../contracts/projection";

import type { LatticeEdgeCategory,LatticeFlowEdge,LatticeFlowNode,LatticeNodeCategory,LatticePinnedPositions,RelationshipFamily,ResolutionState } from "../canvas/lattice-flow-types";
const relationshipLabel=(kind:string,source:string,target:string)=>{if(target.includes("physical-attributes"))return "physical properties";if(target.includes("product-context"))return "product context";if(target.includes("requirements"))return "defined by";if(target.includes("changes"))return "changed through";if(kind==="described-by")return "described by";if(kind==="supersedes")return "supersedes";if(kind==="affected-by")return "affected by";if(kind==="contains"&&target.includes("requirement"))return "contains requirement";if(kind==="requirement")return "defined by";return undefined};
const relationshipFamily=(kind:RelationshipProjection["edges"][number]["kind"]):RelationshipFamily=>kind==="contains"?"structure":kind==="requirement"?"requirement":kind==="business-impact"?"operational":kind==="corresponds-to"||kind==="comparison"?"correspondence":"evidence";
const edgeCategory=(kind:RelationshipProjection["edges"][number]["kind"]):LatticeEdgeCategory=>kind==="contains"?"structure":kind==="corresponds-to"||kind==="comparison"?"correspondence":kind==="requirement"?"requirement":kind==="business-impact"?"operational-impact":"evidence";
function resolution(value?:string):ResolutionState{return value==="failed"?"failed":value==="partial"?"partial":value==="warning"?"warning":value==="resolving"||value==="extracting"?"resolving":"ready"}
export function toReactFlow(projection:RelationshipProjection,positions:Record<string,{x:number;y:number}>,pinned:LatticePinnedPositions={},orientation:LayoutOrientation="RIGHT",layout?:UniversalLayoutResult):{nodes:LatticeFlowNode[];edges:LatticeFlowEdge[]}{
 const branchDirection=new Map(layout?.branches.flatMap(branch=>branch.nodeIds.map(id=>[id,branch.direction] as const))??[]);
 const incoming=new Map<string,RelationshipProjection["edges"]>();const outgoing=new Map<string,RelationshipProjection["edges"]>();
 for(const edge of projection.edges){incoming.set(edge.target,[...(incoming.get(edge.target)??[]),edge]);outgoing.set(edge.source,[...(outgoing.get(edge.source)??[]),edge])}
 const flowNodes=projection.nodes.map((node,index)=>{
  const dimensions=projectedNodeDimensions(node);
  const represented=(incoming.get(node.id)??[]).some(edge=>edge.kind==="represented-by");
  const domain=node.id.startsWith("projection:domain:");
  const category:LatticeNodeCategory=node.level===0?"subject":domain?"cluster":represented?"source-representation":node.kind==="requirement"?"requirement":node.kind==="change-notice"||node.kind==="change-task"?"change":node.kind==="finding"||node.kind==="document"?"evidence":"engineering-item";
  const inheritedSource=node.level>1&&!domain&&node.hasChildren?node.inheritedSource:undefined;
  const attributes=node.attributes??{};
  const completeness=Number(attributes.Completeness??attributes.completeness??(node.resolutionState==="partial"?65:100));
  const families=[...new Set([...(incoming.get(node.id)??[]),...(outgoing.get(node.id)??[])].map(edge=>relationshipFamily(edge.kind)))];
  return {
    id:node.id,
    type:category,
    position:pinned[node.id]??positions[node.id]??{x:node.level*320,y:0},
    origin:[0,0],
    width:dimensions.width,
    height:dimensions.height,
    style:{width:dimensions.width,height:dimensions.height},
    selected:node.selected,
    data:{category,label:node.label,subtitle:node.subtitle,source:node.source,inheritedSource,level:node.level,selected:node.selected,expanded:node.expanded,hasChildren:node.hasChildren,hiddenChildren:node.hiddenChildren,entityKind:node.kind,nativeIdentifier:node.nativeIdentifier,revision:node.revision,completeness,resolutionState:resolution(node.resolutionState),matchConfidence:node.matchConfidence===undefined?undefined:Math.round(node.matchConfidence*100),childCount:node.childCount,lastCapturedAt:node.capturedAt,participatingSystems:node.provenanceSources?.length??1,coverageSummary:`${node.relationshipCount} relationships`,relationshipFamilies:families.length?families:["structure"],orientation,activeResolution:node.resolutionState==="resolving"||node.resolutionState==="extracting",pinned:Boolean(pinned[node.id]),evidenceSummary:node.subtitle,attributes:node.attributes,entering:true,revealIndex:index,geometryRevision:dimensions.width*1000+dimensions.height,routeSettled:true,dimmed:node.dimmed,layoutDirection:branchDirection.get(node.id),radialHandles:layout?Object.values(layout.ports).filter(port=>port.nodeId===node.id&&Boolean(port.branchId)):undefined}
  } as LatticeFlowNode;
});
 const nodeById=new Map(flowNodes.map(node=>[node.id,node]));
 const flowEdges=projection.edges.map(edge=>{
  const category=edgeCategory(edge.kind),family=relationshipFamily(edge.kind);
  const source=nodeById.get(edge.source),target=nodeById.get(edge.target);
  const sourceCenter={x:(source?.position.x??0)+(source?.width??196)/2,y:(source?.position.y??0)+(source?.height??76)/2};
  const targetCenter={x:(target?.position.x??0)+(target?.width??196)/2,y:(target?.position.y??0)+(target?.height??76)/2};
  const dx=targetCenter.x-sourceCenter.x,dy=targetCenter.y-sourceCenter.y;
  const horizontal=Math.abs(dx)>=Math.abs(dy);
  const sourceSide=horizontal?(dx>=0?"east":"west"):(dy>=0?"south":"north");
  const targetSide=horizontal?(dx>=0?"west":"east"):(dy>=0?"north":"south");
  const label=edge.label||relationshipLabel(edge.kind,edge.source,edge.target);
  return {id:edge.id,source:edge.source,target:edge.target,sourceHandle:`source:${sourceSide}:${family}`,targetHandle:`target:${targetSide}:${family}`,type:"lattice-edge",selectable:true,focusable:true,deletable:false,reconnectable:false,selected:edge.selected,interactionWidth:26,ariaLabel:`${edge.fullLabel??label??edge.kind} relationship`,data:{category,relationshipKind:edge.kind,label,fullLabel:edge.fullLabel||label,confidence:edge.confidence,direction:edge.kind==="corresponds-to"?"bidirectional":(edge.confidence??1)<.7?"inferred":"forward",importance:edge.kind==="contains"?"primary":(edge.confidence??1)<.65?"low":"normal",animation:edge.selected?"selected":"settled",authoritative:true,layoutRoute:undefined}} as LatticeFlowEdge;
 });
 return {nodes:flowNodes,edges:flowEdges};
}

import type { LatticeHandoff } from "../contracts/handoff";

  isEngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1,
  type EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1,

export async function investigateSap(
  handoff: LatticeHandoff,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1> {
  const response = await fetch("/api/engineering/intelligence/sap", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ handoff }),
    signal,
  });
  const value = await response.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!response.ok)
    throw new Error(value?.message ?? "SAP intelligence failed.");
  if (!isEngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1(value))
    throw new Error("SAP intelligence returned an invalid canonical contract.");
  return value;
}

===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\adapters\current-canonical-assembly-adapter.ts =====
import type { LatticeHandoff, LatticeSourceEnvelope } from "../../contracts/handoff";
import {
  isEngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1,
  type EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1,
} from "../../contracts/intelligence-v1";
import type { ResolvedStructure } from "../../discovery/structure-resolver";
import { legacyHandoffToIntelligence } from "../../engines/legacy-handoff-to-intelligence";
import { mergeSourceIntelligence } from "../../infrastructure/merge-source-intelligence";
import type { EvidenceEnvelope } from "../contracts/evidence-envelope";
import type { InvestigationSnapshot } from "../contracts/investigation-snapshot";
import type { CanonicalAssemblyPort } from "../ports/canonical-assembly-port";

function isResolvedStructure(value: unknown): value is ResolvedStructure {
  return value !== null && typeof value === "object" && "root" in value && "state" in value;
}

function structureHandoff(
  snapshot: InvestigationSnapshot,
  evidence: readonly EvidenceEnvelope[],
): LatticeHandoff | undefined {
  const sources: LatticeSourceEnvelope[] = [];
  for (const envelope of evidence) {
    if (envelope.identity.operation !== "resolve-structure" || !isResolvedStructure(envelope.payload)) continue;
    const selection = snapshot.selections.find((item) => item.source === envelope.identity.source);
    if (!selection) continue;
    const payload = envelope.payload;
    sources.push({
      source: selection.source as LatticeSourceEnvelope["source"],
      label: selection.source,
      root: payload.root,
      nativeId: selection.nativeId,
      jobId: payload.jobId,
      capturedAt: envelope.observedAt,
      completeness: payload.state === "ready" ? "complete" : "partial",
      intelligencePayload: payload.intelligencePayload,
    });
  }
  if (!sources.length) return undefined;
  return {
    version: 2,
    handoffId: String(snapshot.identity.investigationId),
    createdAt: snapshot.createdAt,
    expiresAt: new Date(Date.parse(snapshot.createdAt) + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),
    subjectLabel: snapshot.subjectLabel,
    sources,
  };
}

export class CurrentCanonicalAssemblyAdapter
  implements CanonicalAssemblyPort<EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1>
{
  async assemble(
    snapshot: InvestigationSnapshot,
    evidence: readonly EvidenceEnvelope[],
  ): Promise<EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1> {
    const intelligence = evidence
      .filter((item) => item.validation.status === "accepted")
      .map((item) => item.payload)
      .filter(isEngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1)
      .sort((left, right) => {
        const leftSource = left.sources[0]?.source ?? "";
        const rightSource = right.sources[0]?.source ?? "";
        return leftSource.localeCompare(rightSource) || left.investigationId.localeCompare(right.investigationId);
      });
    if (intelligence.length) return mergeSourceIntelligence(intelligence);
    const handoff = structureHandoff(snapshot, evidence);
    if (handoff) return legacyHandoffToIntelligence(handoff);
    throw new Error("No accepted evidence is available for current canonical assembly.");
  }
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\adapters\current-evidence-adapter.ts =====
import {
  ENGINEERING_INTELLIGENCE_CONTRACT_VERSION,
  isEngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1,
  type EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1,
  type IntelligenceAuthority,
} from "../../contracts/intelligence-v1";
import type { ResolvedStructure } from "../../discovery/structure-resolver";
import type {
  EvidenceAuthority,
  EvidenceEnvelope,
  EvidenceValidation,
} from "../contracts/evidence-envelope";
import type { ExecutionIdentity } from "../contracts/investigation-identity";
import type { SourceOperationResult } from "../contracts/source-operation-result";

const authorityMap: Readonly<Record<IntelligenceAuthority, EvidenceAuthority>> = {
  "source-authoritative": "source-authoritative",
  "backend-derived": "backend-derived",
  "frontend-derived": "frontend-derived",
  "poc-synthetic": "synthetic",
  hardcoded: "synthetic",
  mocked: "synthetic",
  unavailable: "unavailable",
};

export interface CurrentEvidenceContext {
  identity: ExecutionIdentity;
  observedAt: string;
  warnings?: readonly string[];
}

export function wrapCurrentIntelligenceEvidence(
  value: unknown,
  context: CurrentEvidenceContext,
): EvidenceEnvelope<EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1> {
  const accepted = isEngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1(value);
  const validation: EvidenceValidation = {
    status: accepted ? "accepted" : "rejected",
    schemaVersion: accepted ? ENGINEERING_INTELLIGENCE_CONTRACT_VERSION : "unknown",
    issues: accepted ? [] : ["invalid-engineering-intelligence-contract"],
  };
  if (!accepted) {
    return {
      envelopeVersion: "1.0",
      identity: context.identity,
      authority: "unavailable",
      observedAt: context.observedAt,
      freshness: "unknown",
      validation,
      warnings: context.warnings ?? [],
      payload: value as EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1,
    };
  }
  const authorities = new Set<IntelligenceAuthority>([
    value.subject.provenance.authority,
    ...value.entities.map((item) => item.provenance.authority),
    ...value.relationships.map((item) => item.provenance.authority),
  ]);
  const authority = authorities.has("poc-synthetic") || authorities.has("hardcoded") || authorities.has("mocked")
    ? "synthetic"
    : authorityMap[value.subject.provenance.authority];
  return {
    envelopeVersion: "1.0",
    identity: context.identity,
    authority,
    observedAt: value.temporal.capturedAt ?? context.observedAt,
    freshness: value.temporal.freshness === "not-applicable" ? "unknown" : value.temporal.freshness,
    validation,
    warnings: context.warnings ?? [],
    payload: value,
  };
}

export function wrapCurrentStructureEvidence(
  value: ResolvedStructure,
  context: CurrentEvidenceContext,
): EvidenceEnvelope<ResolvedStructure> {
  const accepted = value.state === "ready" || value.state === "partial";
  return {
    envelopeVersion: "1.0",
    identity: context.identity,
    authority: accepted ? "source-authoritative" : "unavailable",
    observedAt: context.observedAt,
    freshness: "unknown",
    validation: {
      status: accepted ? "accepted" : "rejected",
      schemaVersion: "current-resolved-structure-v1",
      issues: accepted ? [] : [`structure-resolution-${value.state}`],
    },
    warnings: [...(context.warnings ?? []), ...(value.warning ? [value.warning] : [])],
    payload: value,
  };
}

export function rejectedEvidenceResult(
  identity: ExecutionIdentity,
  startedAt: string,
  completedAt: string,
  message: string,
): SourceOperationResult {
  return {
    identity,
    status: "failed",
    startedAt,
    completedAt,
    durationMs: Math.max(0, Date.parse(completedAt) - Date.parse(startedAt)),
    warnings: [],
    error: {
      code: "contract-invalid",
      message,
      retryable: false,
    },
  };
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\adapters\current-source-execution-adapter.ts =====
import type { LatticeHandoff } from "../../contracts/handoff";
import type { NormalizedSearchResult } from "../../discovery/contracts";
import { resolveSelectedStructure } from "../../discovery/structure-resolver";
import { investigateConfigit } from "../../infrastructure/configit-intelligence-client";
import { investigateSap } from "../../infrastructure/sap-intelligence-client";
import { investigateWindchill } from "../../infrastructure/windchill-intelligence-client";
import type { SourceOperationCommand, SourceOperationError } from "../contracts/source-operation";
import type { SourceOperationResult } from "../contracts/source-operation-result";
import type { SourceExecutionPort } from "../ports/source-execution-port";
import {
  rejectedEvidenceResult,
  wrapCurrentIntelligenceEvidence,
  wrapCurrentStructureEvidence,
} from "./current-evidence-adapter";

export interface CurrentHandoffProvider {
  getHandoff(command: SourceOperationCommand): Promise<LatticeHandoff | undefined>;
}

const currentSources = new Set(["sap", "teamcenter", "windchill", "configit"]);

function toSearchResult(command: SourceOperationCommand): NormalizedSearchResult {
  const selection = command.selection;
  if (!currentSources.has(selection.source))
    throw new Error(`Unsupported current source: ${selection.source}`);
  const metadata = Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(selection.metadata ?? {}).map(([key, value]) => [key, value]),
  );
  return {
    resultId: `${selection.source}:${selection.nativeId}`,
    source: selection.source as NormalizedSearchResult["source"],
    nativeId: selection.nativeId,
    displayName: selection.displayName,
    entityType: metadata.entityType ?? "engineering-item",
    matchCategory: "source-native-reference",
    matchReason: "Selected source identity supplied by the Investigation Kernel.",
    capabilityHints: [],
    sourceMetadata: metadata,
    provenance: {
      providerMode: "capability",
      retrievedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      sourceRoute: "investigation-kernel/current-source-execution-adapter",
    },
    canStartInvestigation: true,
  };
}

function operationError(cause: unknown, aborted: boolean): SourceOperationError {
  const message = cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : "Source operation failed.";
  if (aborted)
    return { code: "source-cancelled", message, retryable: false };
  if (/timeout|timed out|exceeded/i.test(message))
    return { code: "source-timeout", message, retryable: true };
  if (/unauthor|forbidden/i.test(message))
    return { code: "authorization-failed", message, retryable: false };
  if (/authentication|sign.?in|login/i.test(message))
    return { code: "authentication-failed", message, retryable: false };
  if (/not found|no .* source|no usable/i.test(message))
    return { code: "not-found", message, retryable: false };
  if (/invalid .*contract|malformed/i.test(message))
    return { code: "contract-invalid", message, retryable: false };
  return { code: "unknown", message, retryable: true };
}

export class CurrentSourceExecutionAdapter implements SourceExecutionPort {
  private readonly resolvedStructures = new Map<string, { value: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof resolveSelectedStructure>>; observedAt: string }>();

  constructor(private readonly handoffs: CurrentHandoffProvider) {}

  private structureKey(source: string, nativeId: string): string {
    return `${source}:${nativeId}`;
  }

  resolvedHandoff(investigationId: string, subjectLabel: string, selections: readonly { source: string; nativeId: string }[]): LatticeHandoff | undefined {
    const sources = selections.flatMap((selection) => {
      const cached = this.resolvedStructures.get(this.structureKey(selection.source, selection.nativeId));
      if (!cached) return [];
      return [{
        source: selection.source as LatticeHandoff["sources"][number]["source"],
        label: selection.source,
        root: cached.value.root,
        nativeId: selection.nativeId,
        jobId: cached.value.jobId,
        capturedAt: cached.observedAt,
        completeness: cached.value.state === "ready" ? "complete" as const : "partial" as const,
        intelligencePayload: cached.value.intelligencePayload,
      }];
    });
    if (!sources.length) return undefined;
    const createdAt = new Date().toISOString();
    return {
      version: 2,
      handoffId: investigationId,
      createdAt,
      expiresAt: new Date(Date.parse(createdAt) + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),
      subjectLabel,
      sources,
    };
  }

  private cachedHandoff(command: SourceOperationCommand): LatticeHandoff | undefined {
    const cached = this.resolvedStructures.get(this.structureKey(command.selection.source, command.selection.nativeId));
    if (!cached) return undefined;
    return {
      version: 2,
      handoffId: String(command.identity.investigationId),
      createdAt: cached.observedAt,
      expiresAt: new Date(Date.parse(cached.observedAt) + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),
      subjectLabel: command.selection.displayName,
      sources: [{
        source: command.selection.source as LatticeHandoff["sources"][number]["source"],
        label: command.selection.source,
        root: cached.value.root,
        nativeId: command.selection.nativeId,
        jobId: cached.value.jobId,
        capturedAt: cached.observedAt,
        completeness: cached.value.state === "ready" ? "complete" : "partial",
        intelligencePayload: cached.value.intelligencePayload,
      }],
    };
  }

  async execute(command: SourceOperationCommand): Promise<SourceOperationResult> {
    const startedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    try {
      if (command.identity.operation === "resolve-structure") {
        const resolved = await resolveSelectedStructure(
          toSearchResult(command),
          command.signal,
        );
        const completedAt = new Date().toISOString();
        if (resolved.state === "ready" || resolved.state === "partial") {
          this.resolvedStructures.set(this.structureKey(command.selection.source, command.selection.nativeId), { value: resolved, observedAt: completedAt });
        }
        const evidence = wrapCurrentStructureEvidence(resolved, {
          identity: command.identity,
          observedAt: completedAt,
          warnings: resolved.warning ? [resolved.warning] : [],
        });
        const status = resolved.state === "ready"
          ? "succeeded"
          : resolved.state === "partial"
            ? "partial"
            : resolved.state;
        return {
          identity: command.identity,
          status,
          startedAt,
          completedAt,
          durationMs: Math.max(0, Date.parse(completedAt) - Date.parse(startedAt)),
          evidence,
          warnings: evidence.warnings,
          error: evidence.validation.status === "rejected"
            ? {
                code: resolved.state === "timed-out" ? "source-timeout" : resolved.state === "cancelled" ? "source-cancelled" : "unknown",
                message: resolved.warning ?? `Structure resolution ${resolved.state}.`,
                retryable: resolved.state === "timed-out" || resolved.state === "failed",
              }
            : undefined,
        };
      }

      const handoff = await this.handoffs.getHandoff(command) ?? this.cachedHandoff(command);
      if (!handoff) {
        const completedAt = new Date().toISOString();
        return rejectedEvidenceResult(
          command.identity,
          startedAt,
          completedAt,
          "Current intelligence execution requires a source handoff.",
        );
      }
      const current = handoff.sources.some((item) => item.source === command.selection.source)
        ? { ...handoff, sources: handoff.sources.filter((item) => item.source === command.selection.source) }
        : handoff;
      const value = command.selection.source === "windchill"
        ? await investigateWindchill(current, command.signal)
        : command.selection.source === "sap"
          ? await investigateSap(current, command.signal)
          : command.selection.source === "configit"
            ? await investigateConfigit(current, command.signal)
            : undefined;
      const completedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      if (!value)
        return {
          identity: command.identity,
          status: "empty",
          startedAt,
          completedAt,
          durationMs: Math.max(0, Date.parse(completedAt) - Date.parse(startedAt)),
          warnings: [`No current intelligence client is registered for ${command.selection.source}.`],
        };
      const evidence = wrapCurrentIntelligenceEvidence(value, {
        identity: command.identity,
        observedAt: completedAt,
      });
      if (evidence.validation.status === "rejected")
        return rejectedEvidenceResult(
          command.identity,
          startedAt,
          completedAt,
          "Current intelligence response failed contract validation.",
        );
      const sourceState = value.sources.find((item) => item.source === command.selection.source)?.status;
      const status = sourceState === "partial" || sourceState === "capability-limited"
        ? "partial"
        : sourceState === "unavailable"
          ? "unavailable"
          : sourceState === "timed-out"
            ? "timed-out"
            : sourceState === "cancelled"
              ? "cancelled"
              : sourceState === "failed"
                ? "failed"
                : "succeeded";
      return {
        identity: command.identity,
        status,
        startedAt,
        completedAt,
        durationMs: Math.max(0, Date.parse(completedAt) - Date.parse(startedAt)),
        evidence,
        warnings: evidence.warnings,
      };
    } catch (cause) {
      const completedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      const error = operationError(cause, command.signal.aborted);
      return {
        identity: command.identity,
        status: error.code === "source-cancelled" ? "cancelled" : error.code === "source-timeout" ? "timed-out" : "failed",
        startedAt,
        completedAt,
        durationMs: Math.max(0, Date.parse(completedAt) - Date.parse(startedAt)),
        warnings: [],
        error,
      };
    }
  }
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\application\canonical-publication-coordinator.ts =====
import type { EvidenceEnvelope } from "../contracts/evidence-envelope";
import type { InvestigationSnapshot } from "../contracts/investigation-snapshot";
import type { CanonicalAssemblyPort } from "../ports/canonical-assembly-port";
import type { InvestigationClock } from "../ports/kernel-services";
import type { InvestigationAuthorityPort } from "../ports/investigation-authority-port";

export class CanonicalPublicationCoordinator<TCanonical = unknown> {
  constructor(
    private readonly kernel: InvestigationAuthorityPort,
    private readonly assembly: CanonicalAssemblyPort<TCanonical>,
    private readonly clock: InvestigationClock,
  ) {}

  async assembleAndPublish(
    snapshot: InvestigationSnapshot,
    evidence: readonly EvidenceEnvelope[],
  ): Promise<InvestigationSnapshot> {
    await this.kernel.dispatch({
      type: "request-assembly",
      investigationId: snapshot.identity.investigationId,
      occurredAt: this.clock.now(),
    });
    try {
      const current = await this.kernel.load(snapshot.identity.investigationId);
      if (!current) throw new Error("Investigation snapshot is unavailable for canonical assembly.");
      const canonical = await this.assembly.assemble(current, evidence);
      return await this.kernel.dispatch({
        type: "publish-canonical",
        investigationId: current.identity.investigationId,
        revision: current.identity.revision,
        canonical,
        occurredAt: this.clock.now(),
      });
    } catch (cause) {
      return await this.kernel.dispatch({
        type: "fail",
        investigationId: snapshot.identity.investigationId,
        code: "canonical-assembly-failed",
        message: cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : "Canonical assembly failed.",
        occurredAt: this.clock.now(),
      });
    }
  }
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\application\completeness-policy.ts =====
import type { CompletenessAssessment } from "../contracts/completeness-assessment";
import type { SourceExecutionSnapshot } from "../contracts/investigation-snapshot";

export function assessCompleteness(
  requestedSources: readonly string[],
  executions: Readonly<Record<string, SourceExecutionSnapshot>>,
): CompletenessAssessment {
  const bySource = new Map<string, SourceExecutionSnapshot[]>();
  for (const execution of Object.values(executions)) {
    const list = bySource.get(execution.identity.source) ?? [];
    list.push(execution);
    bySource.set(execution.identity.source, list);
  }

  const successfulSources: string[] = [];
  const partialSources: string[] = [];
  const failedSources: string[] = [];
  const pendingSources: string[] = [];
  const limitations: string[] = [];

  for (const source of requestedSources) {
    const states = bySource.get(source) ?? [];
    if (!states.length || states.some((item) => item.status === "requested" || item.status === "running")) {
      pendingSources.push(source);
      continue;
    }
    if (states.every((item) => item.status === "succeeded" || item.status === "empty")) {
      successfulSources.push(source);
      continue;
    }
    if (states.some((item) => item.status === "succeeded" || item.status === "partial")) {
      partialSources.push(source);
      limitations.push(`${source} returned partial evidence.`);
      continue;
    }
    failedSources.push(source);
    limitations.push(`${source} evidence is unavailable.`);
  }

  const overall: CompletenessAssessment["overall"] = pendingSources.length
    ? "pending"
    : successfulSources.length || partialSources.length
      ? failedSources.length || partialSources.length
        ? "partial"
        : "complete"
      : "failed";

  return {
    overall,
    requestedSources: [...requestedSources],
    successfulSources,
    partialSources,
    failedSources,
    pendingSources,
    limitations,
  };
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\application\investigation-command-handler.ts =====
import type { InvestigationCommand } from "../contracts/investigation-command";
import type { InvestigationEvent } from "../contracts/investigation-event";
import type { InvestigationSnapshot } from "../contracts/investigation-snapshot";
import type { InvestigationIdProvider } from "../ports/kernel-services";
import { assessExecutionResult, executionKey } from "./stale-result-guard";

export class InvestigationCommandHandler {
  constructor(private readonly ids: InvestigationIdProvider) {}

  handle(
    command: InvestigationCommand,
    snapshot: InvestigationSnapshot | undefined,
    nextSequence: number,
  ): readonly InvestigationEvent[] {
    const metadata = () => ({
      eventId: this.ids.eventId(),
      sequence: nextSequence,
      occurredAt: command.occurredAt,
    });
    switch (command.type) {
      case "create":
        if (snapshot) throw new Error("Investigation already exists.");
        return [{
          ...metadata(),
          type: "investigation-created",
          identity: {
            investigationId: command.investigationId,
            revision: 1,
            executionEpoch: 1,
          },
          subjectLabel: command.subjectLabel,
          query: command.query,
          selections: command.selections,
        }];
      case "begin":
        return [{ ...metadata(), type: "investigation-begun", investigationId: command.investigationId }];
      case "source-requested":
        return [{ ...metadata(), type: "source-operation-requested", identity: command.identity }];
      case "source-started":
        return [{ ...metadata(), type: "source-operation-started", identity: command.identity }];
      case "source-completed": {
        if (!snapshot) throw new Error("Investigation does not exist.");
        const active = snapshot.executions[executionKey(command.result.identity)];
        const decision = assessExecutionResult(snapshot.identity, active, command.result.identity);
        return decision.accepted
          ? [{ ...metadata(), type: "source-operation-completed", result: command.result }]
          : [{
              ...metadata(),
              type: "stale-result-rejected",
              investigationId: snapshot.identity.investigationId,
              identity: command.result.identity,
              reason: decision.reason ?? "stale-result",
            }];
      }
      case "request-assembly":
        return [{ ...metadata(), type: "canonical-assembly-requested", investigationId: command.investigationId }];
      case "publish-canonical":
        return [{
          ...metadata(),
          type: "canonical-published",
          investigationId: command.investigationId,
          revision: command.revision,
          canonical: command.canonical,
        }];
      case "refresh":
        if (!snapshot) throw new Error("Investigation does not exist.");
        return [{
          ...metadata(),
          type: "investigation-refreshed",
          identity: {
            investigationId: snapshot.identity.investigationId,
            revision: snapshot.identity.revision + 1,
            executionEpoch: snapshot.identity.executionEpoch + 1,
          },
        }];
      case "cancel":
        return [{ ...metadata(), type: "investigation-cancelled", investigationId: command.investigationId }];
      case "fail":
        return [{
          ...metadata(),
          type: "investigation-failed",
          investigationId: command.investigationId,
          code: command.code,
          message: command.message,
        }];
    }
  }
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\application\investigation-event-reducer.ts =====
import type { InvestigationEvent } from "../contracts/investigation-event";
import type { InvestigationSnapshot, SourceExecutionSnapshot } from "../contracts/investigation-snapshot";
import { assessCompleteness } from "./completeness-policy";
import { assertTransition } from "./investigation-transition-policy";
import { executionKey } from "./stale-result-guard";

export function reduceInvestigationEvent(
  current: InvestigationSnapshot | undefined,
  event: InvestigationEvent,
): InvestigationSnapshot {
  if (event.type === "investigation-created") {
    if (current) throw new Error("Investigation already exists.");
    return {
      identity: event.identity,
      lifecycle: "draft",
      subjectLabel: event.subjectLabel,
      query: event.query,
      selections: [...event.selections],
      executions: {},
      acceptedEvidenceIds: [],
      completeness: assessCompleteness(event.selections.map((item) => item.source), {}),
      warnings: [],
      createdAt: event.occurredAt,
      updatedAt: event.occurredAt,
    };
  }
  if (!current) throw new Error("Investigation has not been created.");

  const transition = (lifecycle: InvestigationSnapshot["lifecycle"]): InvestigationSnapshot => {
    assertTransition(current.lifecycle, lifecycle);
    return { ...current, lifecycle, updatedAt: event.occurredAt };
  };

  switch (event.type) {
    case "investigation-begun":
      return transition("resolving");
    case "source-operation-requested": {
      const key = executionKey(event.identity);
      const entry: SourceExecutionSnapshot = {
        identity: event.identity,
        status: "requested",
        warnings: [],
      };
      const executions: Readonly<Record<string, SourceExecutionSnapshot>> = {
        ...current.executions,
        [key]: entry,
      };
      return {
        ...current,
        executions,
        completeness: assessCompleteness(current.selections.map((item) => item.source), executions),
        updatedAt: event.occurredAt,
      };
    }
    case "source-operation-started": {
      const key = executionKey(event.identity);
      const prior = current.executions[key];
      if (!prior) return current;
      const entry: SourceExecutionSnapshot = {
        ...prior,
        status: "running",
        startedAt: event.occurredAt,
      };
      const executions: Readonly<Record<string, SourceExecutionSnapshot>> = {
        ...current.executions,
        [key]: entry,
      };
      return {
        ...current,
        lifecycle: event.identity.operation === "resolve-structure" ? "resolving" : "gathering",
        executions,
        completeness: assessCompleteness(current.selections.map((item) => item.source), executions),
        updatedAt: event.occurredAt,
      };
    }
    case "source-operation-completed": {
      const key = executionKey(event.result.identity);
      const prior = current.executions[key];
      if (!prior) return current;
      const entry: SourceExecutionSnapshot = {
        identity: event.result.identity,
        status: event.result.status,
        startedAt: event.result.startedAt,
        completedAt: event.result.completedAt,
        warnings: event.result.warnings,
        errorCode: event.result.error?.code,
        errorMessage: event.result.error?.message,
      };
      const executions: Readonly<Record<string, SourceExecutionSnapshot>> = {
        ...current.executions,
        [key]: entry,
      };
      const evidenceId = event.result.evidence
        ? `${event.result.identity.executionId}:${event.result.identity.operation}`
        : undefined;
      const acceptedEvidenceIds =
        evidenceId && event.result.evidence?.validation.status === "accepted"
          ? [...new Set([...current.acceptedEvidenceIds, evidenceId])]
          : current.acceptedEvidenceIds;
      return {
        ...current,
        executions,
        acceptedEvidenceIds,
        completeness: assessCompleteness(current.selections.map((item) => item.source), executions),
        updatedAt: event.occurredAt,
      };
    }
    case "canonical-assembly-requested":
      return transition("assembling");
    case "canonical-published": { const completeness=assessCompleteness(current.selections.map(x=>x.source),current.executions); const lifecycle=completeness.overall==="complete"?"ready":completeness.overall==="failed"?"failed":"partial"; assertTransition(current.lifecycle,lifecycle); return {...current,lifecycle,canonical:{revision:event.revision,publishedAt:event.occurredAt,value:event.canonical},completeness,updatedAt:event.occurredAt}; }
    case "investigation-refreshed": { assertTransition(current.lifecycle,"refreshing"); return {...current,identity:event.identity,lifecycle:"refreshing",executions:{},acceptedEvidenceIds:[],completeness:assessCompleteness(current.selections.map(x=>x.source),{}),warnings:[],updatedAt:event.occurredAt}; }
    case "investigation-cancelled":
      return transition("cancelled");
    case "investigation-failed": { assertTransition(current.lifecycle,"failed"); return {...current,lifecycle:"failed",warnings:[...current.warnings,`${event.code}: ${event.message}`],updatedAt:event.occurredAt}; }
    case "stale-result-rejected":
      return {...current,warnings:[...current.warnings,`Rejected stale ${event.identity.source}/${event.identity.operation} result: ${event.reason}`],updatedAt:event.occurredAt};
  }
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\application\investigation-kernel.ts =====
import type { InvestigationCommand } from "../contracts/investigation-command";
import type { InvestigationId } from "../contracts/investigation-identity";
import type { InvestigationSnapshot } from "../contracts/investigation-snapshot";
import type { InvestigationEventStore } from "../ports/investigation-event-store";
import type { InvestigationRepository } from "../ports/investigation-repository";
import { reduceInvestigationEvent } from "./investigation-event-reducer";
import { InvestigationCommandHandler } from "./investigation-command-handler";

export type InvestigationSubscriber = (snapshot: InvestigationSnapshot) => void;

export class InvestigationKernel {
  private readonly subscribers = new Map<InvestigationId, Set<InvestigationSubscriber>>();
  private readonly queues = new Map<InvestigationId, Promise<void>>();

  constructor(
    private readonly repository: InvestigationRepository,
    private readonly events: InvestigationEventStore,
    private readonly commands: InvestigationCommandHandler,
  ) {}

  dispatch(command: InvestigationCommand): Promise<InvestigationSnapshot> {
    const id = this.commandInvestigationId(command);
    const prior = this.queues.get(id) ?? Promise.resolve();
    let release!: () => void;
    const gate = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { release = resolve; });
    const queued = prior.then(() => gate);
    this.queues.set(id, queued);
    return prior
      .then(() => this.dispatchSerial(id, command))
      .finally(() => {
        release();
        if (this.queues.get(id) === queued) this.queues.delete(id);
      });
  }

  load(id: InvestigationId): Promise<InvestigationSnapshot | undefined> {
    return this.repository.load(id);
  }

  subscribe(id: InvestigationId, subscriber: InvestigationSubscriber): () => void {
    const set = this.subscribers.get(id) ?? new Set<InvestigationSubscriber>();
    set.add(subscriber);
    this.subscribers.set(id, set);
    return () => {
      set.delete(subscriber);
      if (!set.size) this.subscribers.delete(id);
    };
  }

  private async dispatchSerial(
    id: InvestigationId,
    command: InvestigationCommand,
  ): Promise<InvestigationSnapshot> {
    const [snapshot, stream] = await Promise.all([
      this.repository.load(id),
      this.events.read(id),
    ]);
    const produced = this.commands.handle(command, snapshot, stream.length);
    let next = snapshot;
    for (const event of produced) next = reduceInvestigationEvent(next, event);
    if (!next) throw new Error("Command did not produce an investigation snapshot.");
    await this.events.append(id, produced, stream.length);
    await this.repository.save(next, snapshot?.identity.revision ?? next.identity.revision);
    this.publish(next);
    return next;
  }

  private commandInvestigationId(command: InvestigationCommand): InvestigationId {
    return command.type === "source-requested" || command.type === "source-started"
      ? command.identity.investigationId
      : command.type === "source-completed"
        ? command.result.identity.investigationId
        : command.investigationId;
  }

  private publish(snapshot: InvestigationSnapshot): void {
    for (const subscriber of this.subscribers.get(snapshot.identity.investigationId) ?? [])
      subscriber(snapshot);
  }
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\application\investigation-transition-policy.ts =====
import type { InvestigationLifecycle } from "../contracts/investigation-lifecycle";

const allowed: Readonly<Record<InvestigationLifecycle, readonly InvestigationLifecycle[]>> = {
  draft: ["resolving", "cancelled", "failed"],
  resolving: ["gathering", "assembling", "partial", "failed", "cancelled"],
  gathering: ["assembling", "partial", "failed", "cancelled"],
  assembling: ["ready", "partial", "failed", "cancelled"],
  ready: ["refreshing", "cancelled"],
  partial: ["refreshing", "cancelled"],
  failed: ["refreshing"],
  cancelled: ["refreshing"],
  refreshing: ["resolving", "gathering", "assembling", "partial", "failed", "cancelled"],
};

export const canTransition = (from: InvestigationLifecycle, to: InvestigationLifecycle): boolean =>
  from === to || allowed[from].includes(to);

export const assertTransition = (from: InvestigationLifecycle, to: InvestigationLifecycle): void => {
  if (!canTransition(from, to)) throw new Error(`Invalid investigation transition: ${from} -> ${to}`);
};


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\application\source-execution-coordinator.ts =====
import type { EvidenceEnvelope } from "../contracts/evidence-envelope";
import type { ExecutionIdentity, SourceOperationKind } from "../contracts/investigation-identity";
import type { InvestigationSnapshot } from "../contracts/investigation-snapshot";
import type { SourceOperationCommand, SourceSelection } from "../contracts/source-operation";
import type { InvestigationIdProvider, InvestigationClock } from "../ports/kernel-services";
import type { SourceExecutionPort } from "../ports/source-execution-port";
import type { InvestigationAuthorityPort } from "../ports/investigation-authority-port";

export interface CoordinatedExecution {
  evidence: readonly EvidenceEnvelope[];
  snapshot: InvestigationSnapshot;
}

export class SourceExecutionCoordinator {
  private readonly controllers = new Map<string, AbortController>();

  constructor(
    private readonly kernel: InvestigationAuthorityPort,
    private readonly executor: SourceExecutionPort,
    private readonly ids: InvestigationIdProvider,
    private readonly clock: InvestigationClock,
  ) {}

  async run(snapshot: InvestigationSnapshot): Promise<CoordinatedExecution> {
    await this.kernel.dispatch({
      type: "begin",
      investigationId: snapshot.identity.investigationId,
      occurredAt: this.clock.now(),
    });
    const structures = await Promise.all(snapshot.selections.map((selection) =>
      this.runOperation(snapshot, selection, "resolve-structure"),
    ));
    const intelligence = await Promise.all(snapshot.selections.map((selection) =>
      this.runOperation(snapshot, selection, "gather-intelligence"),
    ));
    const latest = await this.kernel.load(snapshot.identity.investigationId);
    if (!latest) throw new Error("Investigation snapshot disappeared during execution.");
    return {
      evidence: [...structures, ...intelligence].flatMap((result) => result ? [result] : []),
      snapshot: latest,
    };
  }

  cancel(snapshot: InvestigationSnapshot): void {
    for (const [key, controller] of this.controllers) {
      if (key.startsWith(`${snapshot.identity.investigationId}:`)) controller.abort("investigation-cancelled");
    }
  }

  private async runOperation(
    snapshot: InvestigationSnapshot,
    selection: SourceSelection,
    operation: SourceOperationKind,
  ): Promise<EvidenceEnvelope | undefined> {
    const identity: ExecutionIdentity = {
      ...snapshot.identity,
      executionId: this.ids.executionId(),
      correlationId: this.ids.correlationId(),
      source: selection.source,
      operation,
      attempt: 1,
    };
    const key = `${snapshot.identity.investigationId}:${identity.executionId}`;
    const controller = new AbortController();
    this.controllers.set(key, controller);
    const command: SourceOperationCommand = { identity, selection, signal: controller.signal };
    await this.kernel.dispatch({ type: "source-requested", identity, occurredAt: this.clock.now() });
    await this.kernel.dispatch({ type: "source-started", identity, occurredAt: this.clock.now() });
    try {
      const result = await this.executor.execute(command);
      await this.kernel.dispatch({ type: "source-completed", result, occurredAt: this.clock.now() });
      return result.evidence?.validation.status === "accepted" ? result.evidence : undefined;
    } finally {
      this.controllers.delete(key);
    }
  }
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\application\stale-result-guard.ts =====
import type { ExecutionIdentity, InvestigationIdentity } from "../contracts/investigation-identity";
import type { SourceExecutionSnapshot } from "../contracts/investigation-snapshot";

export interface StaleResultDecision {
  accepted: boolean;
  reason?: string;
}

export const executionKey = (identity: ExecutionIdentity): string =>
  [identity.source, identity.operation, identity.attempt].join(":");

export function assessExecutionResult(
  current: InvestigationIdentity,
  active: SourceExecutionSnapshot | undefined,
  incoming: ExecutionIdentity,
): StaleResultDecision {
  if (incoming.investigationId !== current.investigationId)
    return { accepted: false, reason: "investigation-id-mismatch" };
  if (incoming.revision !== current.revision)
    return { accepted: false, reason: "revision-mismatch" };
  if (incoming.executionEpoch !== current.executionEpoch)
    return { accepted: false, reason: "execution-epoch-mismatch" };
  if (!active) return { accepted: false, reason: "execution-not-active" };
  if (active.identity.executionId !== incoming.executionId)
    return { accepted: false, reason: "execution-id-mismatch" };
  if (active.identity.attempt !== incoming.attempt)
    return { accepted: false, reason: "attempt-mismatch" };
  if (active.identity.source !== incoming.source || active.identity.operation !== incoming.operation)
    return { accepted: false, reason: "operation-mismatch" };
  return { accepted: true };
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\contracts\completeness-assessment.ts =====
export interface CompletenessAssessment {
  overall: "complete" | "partial" | "failed" | "pending";
  requestedSources: readonly string[];
  successfulSources: readonly string[];
  partialSources: readonly string[];
  failedSources: readonly string[];
  pendingSources: readonly string[];
  limitations: readonly string[];
}

===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\evidence\current-evidence-boundary.ts =====
import type { EvidenceEnvelope as LegacyEvidenceEnvelope } from "../contracts/evidence-envelope";
import { EvidenceAcceptanceService } from "./evidence-acceptance-service";
import { BasicEvidenceValidator } from "./default-evidence-validator";
import type { EvidenceDecision } from "./evidence-contracts";
import { EvidenceValidationRegistry } from "./evidence-validator";
import { legacyEvidenceToCandidate } from "./current-evidence-compatibility";

export async function validateCurrentEvidence<TPayload>(legacy: LegacyEvidenceEnvelope<TPayload>, receivedAt = new Date().toISOString()): Promise<EvidenceDecision<TPayload>> {
  const candidate = legacyEvidenceToCandidate(legacy, receivedAt);
  const registry = new EvidenceValidationRegistry();
  registry.register(new BasicEvidenceValidator(candidate.schema));
  return new EvidenceAcceptanceService(registry, () => receivedAt).evaluate<TPayload>(candidate);
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\evidence\current-evidence-compatibility.ts =====
import type { EvidenceEnvelope as LegacyEvidenceEnvelope } from "../contracts/evidence-envelope";
import type { EvidenceCandidate, EvidenceCompleteness, EvidenceFreshness } from "./evidence-contracts";

const completenessOf = (value: LegacyEvidenceEnvelope): EvidenceCompleteness => {
  if (value.authority === "unavailable") return "unavailable";
  if (value.warnings.length) return "partial";
  return "complete";
};
const freshnessOf = (value: LegacyEvidenceEnvelope): EvidenceFreshness => value.freshness;

export function legacyEvidenceToCandidate<TPayload>(value: LegacyEvidenceEnvelope<TPayload>, receivedAt = new Date().toISOString()): EvidenceCandidate<TPayload> {
  return {
    identity: value.identity,
    schema: { name: value.identity.operation, version: value.validation.schemaVersion },
    authority: value.authority,
    freshness: freshnessOf(value),
    completeness: completenessOf(value),
    capturedAt: value.observedAt,
    receivedAt,
    provenance: {
      provider: "current-evidence-compatibility-adapter",
      sourceRecord: { source: value.identity.source, nativeId: `${value.identity.source}:${value.identity.operation}` },
      observedAt: value.observedAt,
    },
    payload: value.payload,
    warnings: value.warnings,
  };
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\evidence\default-evidence-validator.ts =====
import type { EvidenceValidator } from "./evidence-validator";
import { issue } from "./evidence-validator";
import type { EvidenceCandidate, EvidenceIssue, EvidenceSchemaIdentity } from "./evidence-contracts";

export class BasicEvidenceValidator implements EvidenceValidator {
  readonly name = "lattice-basic-evidence-validator";
  constructor(readonly schema: EvidenceSchemaIdentity) {}
  validate(candidate: EvidenceCandidate): readonly EvidenceIssue[] {
    const issues: EvidenceIssue[] = [];
    if (!candidate.identity.investigationId) issues.push(issue("missing-investigation-id", "Investigation identity is required.", "identity.investigationId"));
    if (candidate.identity.revision < 1) issues.push(issue("invalid-investigation-revision", "Investigation revision must be positive.", "identity.revision"));
    if (!candidate.identity.executionId) issues.push(issue("missing-execution-id", "Execution identity is required.", "identity.executionId"));
    if (!candidate.identity.source.trim()) issues.push(issue("missing-source", "Evidence source is required.", "identity.source"));
    if (!candidate.provenance.provider.trim()) issues.push(issue("missing-provider", "Evidence provider is required.", "provenance.provider"));
    if (!Number.isFinite(Date.parse(candidate.capturedAt))) issues.push(issue("invalid-captured-at", "capturedAt must be an ISO timestamp.", "capturedAt"));
    if (!Number.isFinite(Date.parse(candidate.receivedAt))) issues.push(issue("invalid-received-at", "receivedAt must be an ISO timestamp.", "receivedAt"));
    if (candidate.payload === undefined) issues.push(issue("missing-payload", "Evidence payload cannot be undefined.", "payload"));
    if (["synthetic", "mocked", "hardcoded"].includes(candidate.authority))
      issues.push(issue("non-authoritative-evidence", `${candidate.authority} evidence is isolated from authoritative evidence.`, "authority", "warning"));
    if (candidate.authority === "unavailable" && candidate.completeness !== "unavailable")
      issues.push(issue("authority-completeness-conflict", "Unavailable evidence must declare unavailable completeness.", "completeness"));
    return issues;
  }
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\evidence\evidence-acceptance-service.ts =====
import { hashEvidencePayload } from "./evidence-hasher";
import { asEvidenceId, type AcceptedEvidenceEnvelope, type EvidenceCandidate, type EvidenceDecision, type EvidenceIssue, type EvidenceValidationResult } from "./evidence-contracts";
import { EvidenceValidationRegistry, issue } from "./evidence-validator";

const evidenceIdFor = async (candidate: EvidenceCandidate, payloadHash: string) => {
  const identity = `${candidate.identity.investigationId}|${candidate.identity.revision}|${candidate.identity.executionId}|${candidate.identity.source}|${candidate.identity.operation}|${candidate.schema.name}|${candidate.schema.version}|${payloadHash}`;
  return asEvidenceId(`evidence:${(await hashEvidencePayload(identity)).replace("sha256:", "")}`);
};
const freeze = <T>(value: T): T => {
  if (value && typeof value === "object" && !Object.isFrozen(value)) {
    Object.freeze(value);
    for (const nested of Object.values(value as Record<string, unknown>)) freeze(nested);
  }
  return value;
};

export class EvidenceAcceptanceService {
  constructor(private readonly validators: EvidenceValidationRegistry, private readonly now: () => string = () => new Date().toISOString()) {}

  async evaluate<TPayload>(candidate: EvidenceCandidate<TPayload>): Promise<EvidenceDecision<TPayload>> {
    let payloadHash;
    const issues: EvidenceIssue[] = [];
    try { payloadHash = await hashEvidencePayload(candidate.payload); }
    catch (error) {
      payloadHash = await hashEvidencePayload({ hashingFailure: true });
      issues.push(issue("payload-not-canonicalizable", error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Evidence payload cannot be canonicalized.", "payload"));
    }
    const validator = this.validators.resolve(candidate.schema);
    if (!validator) issues.push(issue("validator-not-registered", `No validator is registered for ${candidate.schema.name}@${candidate.schema.version}.`, "schema"));
    else issues.push(...await validator.validate(candidate as EvidenceCandidate));
    const evidenceId = await evidenceIdFor(candidate, payloadHash);
    const validation: EvidenceValidationResult = {
      status: issues.some((item) => item.severity === "error") ? "rejected" : "accepted",
      schema: candidate.schema,
      validatedAt: this.now(),
      validator: validator?.name ?? "unregistered",
      issues,
    };
    const common = {
      evidenceId,
      investigationId: candidate.identity.investigationId,
      execution: candidate.identity,
      schema: candidate.schema,
      authority: candidate.authority,
      payloadHash,
      receivedAt: candidate.receivedAt,
      provenance: candidate.provenance,
      validation,
      warnings: [...candidate.warnings],
      payload: candidate.payload,
    };
    if (validation.status === "rejected") return freeze({ status: "rejected", ...common, validation: { ...validation, status: "rejected" as const } });
    const evidence: AcceptedEvidenceEnvelope<TPayload> = freeze({
      envelopeVersion: "2.0",
      ...common,
      investigationRevision: candidate.identity.revision,
      capturedAt: candidate.capturedAt,
      freshness: candidate.freshness,
      completeness: candidate.completeness,
      validation: { ...validation, status: "accepted" },
    });
    return { status: "accepted", evidence };
  }

  async verify(envelope: AcceptedEvidenceEnvelope): Promise<boolean> {
    return await hashEvidencePayload(envelope.payload) === envelope.payloadHash;
  }
}
