import type { ExecutionIdentity, InvestigationId } from "../contracts/investigation-identity";

export const IMMUTABLE_EVIDENCE_ENVELOPE_VERSION = "2.0" as const;

export type EvidenceId = string & { readonly __brand: "EvidenceId" };
export type EvidencePayloadHash = string & { readonly __brand: "EvidencePayloadHash" };
export const asEvidenceId = (value: string): EvidenceId => value as EvidenceId;
export const asEvidencePayloadHash = (value: string): EvidencePayloadHash => value as EvidencePayloadHash;

export type EvidenceAuthority =
  | "source-authoritative"
  | "source-cached"
  | "backend-derived"
  | "frontend-derived"
  | "synthetic"
  | "mocked"
  | "hardcoded"
  | "unavailable";

export type EvidenceFreshness = "current" | "stale" | "unknown" | "not-applicable";
export type EvidenceCompleteness = "complete" | "partial" | "empty" | "unavailable";
export type EvidenceValidationStatus = "accepted" | "rejected";
export type EvidenceIssueSeverity = "error" | "warning";

export interface EvidenceIssue {
  readonly code: string;
  readonly message: string;
  readonly path?: string;
  readonly severity: EvidenceIssueSeverity;
}

export interface EvidenceSchemaIdentity {
  readonly name: string;
  readonly version: string;
}

export interface EvidenceSourceRecordIdentity {
  readonly source: string;
  readonly nativeId: string;
  readonly nativeRevision?: string;
}

export interface EvidenceArtifactReference {
  readonly artifactId: string;
  readonly mediaType?: string;
  readonly location?: string;
  readonly byteLength?: number;
}

export interface EvidenceProvenance {
  readonly provider: string;
  readonly sourceRoute?: string;
  readonly sourceRecord?: EvidenceSourceRecordIdentity;
  readonly artifact?: EvidenceArtifactReference;
  readonly observedAt: string;
}

export interface EvidenceValidationResult {
  readonly status: EvidenceValidationStatus;
  readonly schema: EvidenceSchemaIdentity;
  readonly validatedAt: string;
  readonly validator: string;
  readonly issues: readonly EvidenceIssue[];
}

export interface EvidenceCandidate<TPayload = unknown> {
  readonly identity: ExecutionIdentity;
  readonly schema: EvidenceSchemaIdentity;
  readonly authority: EvidenceAuthority;
  readonly freshness: EvidenceFreshness;
  readonly completeness: EvidenceCompleteness;
  readonly capturedAt: string;
  readonly receivedAt: string;
  readonly provenance: EvidenceProvenance;
  readonly payload: TPayload;
  readonly warnings: readonly string[];
}

export interface ImmutableEvidenceEnvelope<TPayload = unknown> {
  readonly envelopeVersion: typeof IMMUTABLE_EVIDENCE_ENVELOPE_VERSION;
  readonly evidenceId: EvidenceId;
  readonly investigationId: InvestigationId;
  readonly investigationRevision: number;
  readonly execution: ExecutionIdentity;
  readonly schema: EvidenceSchemaIdentity;
  readonly authority: EvidenceAuthority;
  readonly freshness: EvidenceFreshness;
  readonly completeness: EvidenceCompleteness;
  readonly capturedAt: string;
  readonly receivedAt: string;
  readonly payloadHash: EvidencePayloadHash;
  readonly provenance: EvidenceProvenance;
  readonly validation: EvidenceValidationResult;
  readonly warnings: readonly string[];
  readonly payload: TPayload;
}

export type AcceptedEvidenceEnvelope<TPayload = unknown> = ImmutableEvidenceEnvelope<TPayload> & {
  readonly validation: EvidenceValidationResult & { readonly status: "accepted" };
};

export interface EvidenceRejection<TPayload = unknown> {
  readonly status: "rejected";
  readonly evidenceId: EvidenceId;
  readonly investigationId: InvestigationId;
  readonly execution: ExecutionIdentity;
  readonly schema: EvidenceSchemaIdentity;
  readonly authority: EvidenceAuthority;
  readonly payloadHash: EvidencePayloadHash;
  readonly receivedAt: string;
  readonly provenance: EvidenceProvenance;
  readonly validation: EvidenceValidationResult & { readonly status: "rejected" };
  readonly warnings: readonly string[];
  readonly payload: TPayload;
}

export type EvidenceDecision<TPayload = unknown> =
  | { readonly status: "accepted"; readonly evidence: AcceptedEvidenceEnvelope<TPayload> }
  | EvidenceRejection<TPayload>;

export interface EvidenceSet {
  readonly investigationId: InvestigationId;
  readonly investigationRevision: number;
  readonly evidenceIds: readonly EvidenceId[];
  readonly rejectedEvidenceIds: readonly EvidenceId[];
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\evidence\evidence-hasher.ts =====
import { asEvidencePayloadHash, type EvidencePayloadHash } from "./evidence-contracts";
import { stableJson } from "./stable-json";

export async function hashEvidencePayload(payload: unknown): Promise<EvidencePayloadHash> {
  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(stableJson(payload));
  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", bytes);
  return asEvidencePayloadHash(`sha256:${[...new Uint8Array(digest)].map((value) => value.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("")}`);
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\evidence\evidence-validator.ts =====
import type { EvidenceCandidate, EvidenceIssue, EvidenceSchemaIdentity } from "./evidence-contracts";

export interface EvidenceValidator<TPayload = unknown> {
  readonly schema: EvidenceSchemaIdentity;
  readonly name: string;
  validate(candidate: EvidenceCandidate<TPayload>): readonly EvidenceIssue[] | Promise<readonly EvidenceIssue[]>;
}

export class EvidenceValidationRegistry {
  private readonly validators = new Map<string, EvidenceValidator>();
  register(validator: EvidenceValidator): void {
    const key = this.key(validator.schema);
    if (this.validators.has(key)) throw new Error(`Evidence validator already registered for ${key}.`);
    this.validators.set(key, validator);
  }
  resolve(schema: EvidenceSchemaIdentity): EvidenceValidator | undefined {
    return this.validators.get(this.key(schema));
  }
  private key(schema: EvidenceSchemaIdentity): string { return `${schema.name}@${schema.version}`; }
}

export const issue = (code: string, message: string, path?: string, severity: EvidenceIssue["severity"] = "error"): EvidenceIssue => ({
  code, message, path, severity,
});

===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\evidence\stable-json.ts =====
function normalized(value: unknown, seen: WeakSet<object>): unknown {
  if (value === null || typeof value === "string" || typeof value === "boolean") return value;
  if (typeof value === "number") return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : String(value);
  if (typeof value === "bigint") return value.toString();
  if (value instanceof Date) return value.toISOString();
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map((item) => normalized(item, seen));
  if (typeof value === "object") {
    if (seen.has(value)) throw new Error("Evidence payload contains a circular reference.");
    seen.add(value);
    const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
    const output = Object.fromEntries(
      Object.keys(record).sort().flatMap((key) => {
        const current = record[key];
        return current === undefined || typeof current === "function" || typeof current === "symbol"
          ? []
          : [[key, normalized(current, seen)]];
      }),
    );
    seen.delete(value);
    return output;
  }
  return String(value);
}

export function stableJson(value: unknown): string {
  return JSON.stringify(normalized(value, new WeakSet<object>()));
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\evidence\storage\evidence-storage-utils.ts =====
import { promises as fs } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
export const evidenceRoot = path.resolve(process.env.LATTICE_EVIDENCE_DATA_DIR?.trim() || path.join(process.cwd(), ".lattice-data", "evidence"));
export const safeSegment = (value: string) => value.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]/g, "_");
export async function readJson<T>(file: string): Promise<T | undefined> { try { return JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(file, "utf8")) as T; } catch (e) { if ((e as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === "ENOENT") return undefined; throw e; } }
export async function atomicJson(file: string, value: unknown): Promise<void> { await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true }); const temp=`${file}.${process.pid}.${crypto.randomUUID()}.tmp`; await fs.writeFile(temp, JSON.stringify(value), "utf8"); await fs.rename(temp,file); }
export async function atomicBytes(file: string, value: Uint8Array): Promise<void> { await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true }); const temp=`${file}.${process.pid}.${crypto.randomUUID()}.tmp`; await fs.writeFile(temp,value); await fs.rename(temp,file); }


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\evidence\storage\file-evidence-artifact-store.ts =====
import { promises as fs } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import type { EvidenceArtifact, EvidenceArtifactStore } from "../../ports/evidence-artifact-store";
import { atomicBytes, atomicJson, evidenceRoot, readJson, safeSegment } from "./evidence-storage-utils";
export class FileEvidenceArtifactStore implements EvidenceArtifactStore {
  private base(id:string){ return path.join(evidenceRoot,"artifacts",safeSegment(id)); }
  async put(a:EvidenceArtifact):Promise<"created"|"existing"> { const base=this.base(a.artifactId); try { await fs.access(`${base}.bin`); return "existing"; } catch {} await atomicBytes(`${base}.bin`,a.bytes); await atomicJson(`${base}.meta.json`,{artifactId:a.artifactId,mediaType:a.mediaType,byteLength:a.bytes.byteLength}); return "created"; }
  async get(id:string):Promise<EvidenceArtifact|undefined>{ const meta=await readJson<{artifactId:string;mediaType:string}>(`${this.base(id)}.meta.json`); if(!meta)return undefined; try{return{...meta,bytes:new Uint8Array(await fs.readFile(`${this.base(id)}.bin`))};}catch(e){if((e as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code==="ENOENT")return undefined;throw e;} }
}

import path from "node:path";
import type { AcceptedEvidenceEnvelope, EvidenceId, EvidenceRejection } from "../evidence-contracts";
import type { EvidenceManifestEntry, EvidenceQuery, EvidenceRepository } from "../../ports/evidence-repository";
import { hashEvidencePayload } from "../evidence-hasher";
import { atomicJson, evidenceRoot, readJson, safeSegment } from "./evidence-storage-utils";

interface Manifest { version:1; entries:EvidenceManifestEntry[] }
export class EvidenceIntegrityError extends Error {}
export class FileEvidenceRepository implements EvidenceRepository {
  private record(id:string,status:"accepted"|"rejected"){return path.join(evidenceRoot,"records",status,`${safeSegment(id)}.json`);}
  private manifest(id:string,rev:number){return path.join(evidenceRoot,"investigations",safeSegment(id),String(rev),"manifest.json");}
  async putAccepted(e:AcceptedEvidenceEnvelope){return this.put(e,"accepted");}
  async putRejected(e:EvidenceRejection){return this.put(e,"rejected");}
  private async put(e:AcceptedEvidenceEnvelope|EvidenceRejection,status:"accepted"|"rejected"):Promise<"created"|"existing">{
    const file=this.record(e.evidenceId,status); const existing=await readJson<typeof e>(file);
    if(existing){if(existing.payloadHash!==e.payloadHash)throw new EvidenceIntegrityError(`Evidence ${e.evidenceId} is immutable and already exists with another hash.`);return "existing";}
    await atomicJson(file,e); const revision="investigationRevision" in e?e.investigationRevision:e.execution.revision; const mf=this.manifest(e.investigationId,revision); const current=await readJson<Manifest>(mf)??{version:1 as const,entries:[]};
    const entry:EvidenceManifestEntry={evidenceId:e.evidenceId,investigationId:e.investigationId,investigationRevision:revision,executionId:String(e.execution.executionId),source:e.execution.source,operation:e.execution.operation,authority:e.authority,schemaName:e.schema.name,schemaVersion:e.schema.version,payloadHash:e.payloadHash,status,storedAt:new Date().toISOString()};
    await atomicJson(mf,{version:1,entries:[...current.entries.filter(x=>x.evidenceId!==e.evidenceId),entry]}); return "created";
  }
  async getAccepted(id:EvidenceId){const e=await readJson<AcceptedEvidenceEnvelope>(this.record(id,"accepted"));if(e&&await hashEvidencePayload(e.payload)!==e.payloadHash)throw new EvidenceIntegrityError(`Evidence ${id} failed payload hash verification.`);return e;}
  async getRejected(id:EvidenceId){return readJson<EvidenceRejection>(this.record(id,"rejected"));}
  async query(q:EvidenceQuery){ const revisions=q.revision?[q.revision]:await this.revisions(q.investigationId); const all=(await Promise.all(revisions.map(async r=>(await readJson<Manifest>(this.manifest(q.investigationId,r)))?.entries??[]))).flat(); return all.filter(x=>(!q.executionId||x.executionId===q.executionId)&&(!q.source||x.source===q.source)).sort((a,b)=>a.storedAt.localeCompare(b.storedAt)); }
  private async revisions(id:string):Promise<number[]>{const {promises:fs}=await import("node:fs");const dir=path.join(evidenceRoot,"investigations",safeSegment(id));try{return(await fs.readdir(dir,{withFileTypes:true})).filter(x=>x.isDirectory()&&/^\d+$/.test(x.name)).map(x=>Number(x.name)).sort((a,b)=>a-b);}catch(e){if((e as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code==="ENOENT")return[];throw e;}}
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\infrastructure\browser-investigation-event-store.ts =====
"use client";

import type { InvestigationEvent } from "../contracts/investigation-event";
import type { InvestigationId } from "../contracts/investigation-identity";
import type { InvestigationEventStore } from "../ports/investigation-event-store";

const PREFIX = "lattice-kernel:events:";

export class BrowserInvestigationEventStore implements InvestigationEventStore {
  async append(id: InvestigationId, events: readonly InvestigationEvent[], expectedSequence: number): Promise<void> {
    const current = await this.read(id);
    if (current.length !== expectedSequence) {
      throw new Error(`Event sequence conflict: expected ${expectedSequence}, found ${current.length}.`);
    }
    localStorage.setItem(`${PREFIX}${id}`, JSON.stringify([...current, ...events]));
  }

  async read(id: InvestigationId): Promise<readonly InvestigationEvent[]> {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(`${PREFIX}${id}`);
      return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) as InvestigationEvent[] : [];
    } catch {
      return [];
    }
  }
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\infrastructure\browser-investigation-repository.ts =====
"use client";

import type { InvestigationId } from "../contracts/investigation-identity";
import type { InvestigationSnapshot } from "../contracts/investigation-snapshot";
import type { InvestigationRepository } from "../ports/investigation-repository";

const PREFIX = "lattice-kernel:snapshot:";

export class BrowserInvestigationRepository implements InvestigationRepository {
  async load<TCanonical = unknown>(id: InvestigationId): Promise<InvestigationSnapshot<TCanonical> | undefined> {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(`${PREFIX}${id}`);
      return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) as InvestigationSnapshot<TCanonical> : undefined;
    } catch {
      return undefined;
    }
  }

  async save<TCanonical = unknown>(snapshot: InvestigationSnapshot<TCanonical>, expectedRevision: number): Promise<void> {
    const current = await this.load(snapshot.identity.investigationId);
    if (current && current.identity.revision !== expectedRevision) {
      throw new Error(`Investigation revision conflict: expected ${expectedRevision}, found ${current.identity.revision}.`);
    }
    localStorage.setItem(`${PREFIX}${snapshot.identity.investigationId}`, JSON.stringify(snapshot));
  }
}

===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\infrastructure\current-investigation-runtime.ts =====
"use client";

import type { LatticeHandoff } from "../../contracts/handoff";
import { isEngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1, type EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1 } from "../../contracts/intelligence-v1";
import type { NormalizedSearchResult } from "../../discovery/contracts";
import { CurrentCanonicalAssemblyAdapter } from "../adapters/current-canonical-assembly-adapter";
import { CurrentSourceExecutionAdapter } from "../adapters/current-source-execution-adapter";
import { CanonicalPublicationCoordinator } from "../application/canonical-publication-coordinator";
import { SourceExecutionCoordinator } from "../application/source-execution-coordinator";
import type { InvestigationSnapshot } from "../contracts/investigation-snapshot";
import { BrowserInvestigationClock, BrowserInvestigationIdProvider } from "./browser-kernel-services";
import { HttpInvestigationAuthority } from "./http-investigation-authority";

export interface KernelInvestigationResult {
  snapshot: InvestigationSnapshot<EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1>;
  canonical: EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1;
  handoff: LatticeHandoff;
}

export function createCurrentInvestigationRuntime(handoffProvider?: (source: string) => Promise<LatticeHandoff | undefined>) {
  const ids = new BrowserInvestigationIdProvider();
  const clock = new BrowserInvestigationClock();
  const kernel = new HttpInvestigationAuthority();
  const executor = new CurrentSourceExecutionAdapter({
    getHandoff: async (command) => handoffProvider?.(command.selection.source),
  });
  const sourceCoordinator = new SourceExecutionCoordinator(kernel, executor, ids, clock);
  const publication = new CanonicalPublicationCoordinator<EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1>(kernel, new CurrentCanonicalAssemblyAdapter(), clock);

  return {
    kernel,
    async investigate(selected: readonly NormalizedSearchResult[], subjectLabel: string): Promise<KernelInvestigationResult> {
      let snapshot = await kernel.create(
        subjectLabel,
        subjectLabel,
        selected.map((item) => ({
          source: item.source,
          nativeId: item.nativeId,
          displayName: item.displayName,
          metadata: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(item.sourceMetadata).flatMap(([key, value]) => value === null ? [] : [[key, String(value)]])),
        })),
      );
      const investigationId = snapshot.identity.investigationId;
      const execution = await sourceCoordinator.run(snapshot);
      snapshot = await publication.assembleAndPublish(execution.snapshot, execution.evidence);
      const canonical = snapshot.canonical?.value;
      if (!isEngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1(canonical)) {
        throw new Error(snapshot.warnings.at(-1) ?? "Investigation Kernel did not publish a valid canonical result.");
      }
      const handoff = executor.resolvedHandoff(String(investigationId), subjectLabel, snapshot.selections);
      if (!handoff) throw new Error("Investigation Kernel resolved no source structures.");
      return { snapshot: snapshot as InvestigationSnapshot<EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1>, canonical, handoff };
    },
  };
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\infrastructure\http-investigation-authority.ts =====
"use client";

import type { InvestigationCommand } from "../contracts/investigation-command";
import { asInvestigationId, type InvestigationId } from "../contracts/investigation-identity";
import type { InvestigationSnapshot } from "../contracts/investigation-snapshot";
import type { SourceSelection } from "../contracts/source-operation";
import type { InvestigationAuthorityPort } from "../ports/investigation-authority-port";

export class InvestigationAuthorityError extends Error {
  constructor(readonly status: number, message: string) { super(message); }
}

async function read<T>(response: Response): Promise<T> {
  const value = await response.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!response.ok) throw new InvestigationAuthorityError(response.status, value?.message ?? "Investigation authority request failed.");
  return value as T;
}

export class HttpInvestigationAuthority implements InvestigationAuthorityPort {
  constructor(private readonly baseUrl = "/api/lattice/investigations") {}

  async create(subjectLabel: string, query: string, selections: readonly SourceSelection[]): Promise<InvestigationSnapshot> {
    const response = await fetch(this.baseUrl, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ subjectLabel, query, selections }),
      cache: "no-store",
    });
    return read<InvestigationSnapshot>(response);
  }

  async dispatch(command: InvestigationCommand): Promise<InvestigationSnapshot> {
    if (command.type === "create") throw new Error("Creation must be requested from the server-owned create endpoint.");
    const id = investigationIdOf(command);
    const response = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ command }),
      cache: "no-store",
    });
    return read<InvestigationSnapshot>(response);
  }

  async load(id: InvestigationId): Promise<InvestigationSnapshot | undefined> {
    const response = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, { cache: "no-store" });
    if (response.status === 404) return undefined;
    return read<InvestigationSnapshot>(response);
  }
}

function investigationIdOf(command: Exclude<InvestigationCommand, { type: "create" }>): InvestigationId {
  if (command.type === "source-requested" || command.type === "source-started") return command.identity.investigationId;
  if (command.type === "source-completed") return command.result.identity.investigationId;
  return asInvestigationId(command.investigationId);
}

# Lattice Investigation Kernel - Phase 1

This package establishes the first executable slicing of Architecture Change #1 without changing the current UI, graph, layout, source routes, or extractors.

Included:

- Investigation and execution identity contracts
- Lifecycle and transition policy
- Source operation contracts
- Evidence envelope
- Completeness policy
- Stale-result guard
- Event reducer
- Repository and execution ports
- Deterministic in-memory testing adapters

Not yet integrated in this phase:

- Current source clients
- Current canonical assembly
- Browser persistence adapters
- React provider
- Workspace migration

The next phase wraps the current runtime behind the ports provided here.


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\server\file-investigation-store.ts =====
import { promises as fs } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import type { InvestigationEvent } from "../contracts/investigation-event";
import type { InvestigationId } from "../contracts/investigation-identity";
import type { InvestigationSnapshot } from "../contracts/investigation-snapshot";
import type { InvestigationEventStore } from "../ports/investigation-event-store";
import type { InvestigationRepository } from "../ports/investigation-repository";

const root = path.resolve(process.env.LATTICE_INVESTIGATION_DATA_DIR?.trim() || path.join(process.cwd(), ".lattice-data", "investigations"));
const safeId = (id: InvestigationId) => String(id).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]/g, "_");
const snapshotPath = (id: InvestigationId) => path.join(root, safeId(id), "snapshot.json");
const eventsPath = (id: InvestigationId) => path.join(root, safeId(id), "events.json");

async function json<T>(file: string, fallback: T): Promise<T> {
  try { return JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(file, "utf8")) as T; }
  catch (error) { if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === "ENOENT") return fallback; throw error; }
}
async function atomic(file: string, value: unknown): Promise<void> {
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true });
  const temporary = `${file}.${process.pid}.${crypto.randomUUID()}.tmp`;
  await fs.writeFile(temporary, JSON.stringify(value), "utf8");
  await fs.rename(temporary, file);
}

export class FileInvestigationRepository implements InvestigationRepository {
  async load<TCanonical = unknown>(id: InvestigationId): Promise<InvestigationSnapshot<TCanonical> | undefined> {
    return json<InvestigationSnapshot<TCanonical> | undefined>(snapshotPath(id), undefined);
  }
  async save<TCanonical = unknown>(snapshot: InvestigationSnapshot<TCanonical>, expectedRevision: number): Promise<void> {
    const current = await this.load(snapshot.identity.investigationId);
    if (current && current.identity.revision !== expectedRevision)
      throw new Error(`Investigation revision conflict: expected ${expectedRevision}, found ${current.identity.revision}.`);
    await atomic(snapshotPath(snapshot.identity.investigationId), snapshot);
  }
}

export class FileInvestigationEventStore implements InvestigationEventStore {
  async append(id: InvestigationId, events: readonly InvestigationEvent[], expectedSequence: number): Promise<void> {
    const current = await this.read(id);
    if (current.length !== expectedSequence)
      throw new Error(`Event sequence conflict: expected ${expectedSequence}, found ${current.length}.`);
    await atomic(eventsPath(id), [...current, ...events]);
  }
  async read(id: InvestigationId): Promise<readonly InvestigationEvent[]> {
    return json<InvestigationEvent[]>(eventsPath(id), []);
  }
}

import type { CorrelationId, ExecutionId, InvestigationId } from "../contracts/investigation-identity";
import { asCorrelationId, asExecutionId, asInvestigationId } from "../contracts/investigation-identity";
import type { InvestigationClock, InvestigationIdProvider } from "../ports/kernel-services";

export class DeterministicClock implements InvestigationClock {
  constructor(private value = "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z") {}
  now(): string { return this.value; }
  set(value: string): void { this.value = value; }
}

export class DeterministicIdProvider implements InvestigationIdProvider {
  private sequence = 0;
  investigationId(): InvestigationId { return asInvestigationId(`investigation-${++this.sequence}`); }
  executionId(): ExecutionId { return asExecutionId(`execution-${++this.sequence}`); }
  correlationId(): CorrelationId { return asCorrelationId(`correlation-${++this.sequence}`); }
  eventId(): string { return `event-${++this.sequence}`; }
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\testing\evidence-model.test.ts =====
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import test from "node:test";
import { asCorrelationId, asExecutionId, asInvestigationId } from "../contracts/investigation-identity";
import { BasicEvidenceValidator } from "../evidence/default-evidence-validator";
import { EvidenceAcceptanceService } from "../evidence/evidence-acceptance-service";
import { hashEvidencePayload } from "../evidence/evidence-hasher";
import { EvidenceValidationRegistry } from "../evidence/evidence-validator";
import type { EvidenceCandidate } from "../evidence/evidence-contracts";

const schema = { name: "engineering-intelligence", version: "1.0" } as const;
const identity = {
  investigationId: asInvestigationId("investigation-1"), revision: 1, executionEpoch: 1,
  executionId: asExecutionId("execution-1"), correlationId: asCorrelationId("correlation-1"),
  source: "windchill", operation: "gather-intelligence" as const, attempt: 1,
};
const candidate = (payload: unknown = { b: 2, a: 1 }): EvidenceCandidate => ({
  identity, schema, authority: "source-authoritative", freshness: "current", completeness: "complete",
  capturedAt: "2026-10-13T00:00:00.000Z", receivedAt: "2026-10-13T00:00:01.000Z",
  provenance: { provider: "test", observedAt: "2026-10-13T00:00:00.000Z", sourceRecord: { source: "windchill", nativeId: "WTPart-1" } },
  payload, warnings: [],
});
const service = () => {
  const registry = new EvidenceValidationRegistry();
  registry.register(new BasicEvidenceValidator(schema));
  return new EvidenceAcceptanceService(registry, () => "2026-10-13T00:00:02.000Z");
};

test("stable hashing ignores object key order", async () => {
  assert.equal(await hashEvidencePayload({ a: 1, b: 2 }), await hashEvidencePayload({ b: 2, a: 1 }));
});
test("accepted evidence has deterministic identity, hash and immutable payload", async () => {
  const first = await service().evaluate(candidate());
  const second = await service().evaluate(candidate());
  assert.equal(first.status, "accepted"); assert.equal(second.status, "accepted");
  if (first.status !== "accepted" || second.status !== "accepted") return;
  assert.equal(first.evidence.evidenceId, second.evidence.evidenceId);
  assert.equal(first.evidence.payloadHash, second.evidence.payloadHash);
  assert.equal(first.evidence.investigationRevision, 1);
  assert.equal(Object.isFrozen(first.evidence), true);
  assert.equal(Object.isFrozen(first.evidence.payload), true);
});
test("unregistered schemas are rejected", async () => {
  const result = await new EvidenceAcceptanceService(new EvidenceValidationRegistry()).evaluate(candidate());
  assert.equal(result.status, "rejected");
  assert.equal(result.validation.issues.some((item) => item.code === "validator-not-registered"), true);
});
test("unavailable authority cannot claim complete evidence", async () => {
  const value = { ...candidate(), authority: "unavailable" as const, completeness: "complete" as const };
  const result = await service().evaluate(value);
  assert.equal(result.status, "rejected");
});

import type { EvidenceEnvelope } from "../contracts/evidence-envelope";
import type { InvestigationSnapshot } from "../contracts/investigation-snapshot";
import type { CanonicalAssemblyPort } from "../ports/canonical-assembly-port";

export class FakeCanonicalAssemblyAdapter<TCanonical = unknown>
  implements CanonicalAssemblyPort<TCanonical>
{
  readonly calls: Array<{ snapshot: InvestigationSnapshot; evidence: readonly EvidenceEnvelope[] }> = [];
  constructor(private readonly value: TCanonical) {}
  async assemble(snapshot: InvestigationSnapshot, evidence: readonly EvidenceEnvelope[]): Promise<TCanonical> {
    this.calls.push({ snapshot, evidence });
    return this.value;
  }
}

import type { EvidenceEnvelope } from "../contracts/evidence-envelope";
import type { SourceOperationCommand } from "../contracts/source-operation";
import type { SourceOperationResult } from "../contracts/source-operation-result";
import type { SourceExecutionPort } from "../ports/source-execution-port";

export type FakeSourceBehavior = (
  command: SourceOperationCommand,
) => Promise<SourceOperationResult>;

export class FakeSourceExecutionAdapter implements SourceExecutionPort {
  readonly commands: SourceOperationCommand[] = [];
  constructor(private readonly behavior: FakeSourceBehavior) {}
  async execute(command: SourceOperationCommand): Promise<SourceOperationResult> {
    this.commands.push(command);
    return this.behavior(command);
  }

  static successful(payload: unknown = {}): FakeSourceExecutionAdapter {
    return new FakeSourceExecutionAdapter(async (command) => {
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      const evidence: EvidenceEnvelope = {
        envelopeVersion: "1.0",
        identity: command.identity,
        authority: "source-authoritative",
        observedAt: now,
        freshness: "current",
        validation: { status: "accepted", schemaVersion: "fake-v1", issues: [] },
        warnings: [],
        payload,
      };
      return {
        identity: command.identity,
        status: "succeeded",
        startedAt: now,
        completedAt: now,
        durationMs: 0,
        evidence,
        warnings: [],
      };
    });
  }
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\testing\in-memory-event-store.ts =====
import type { InvestigationEvent } from "../contracts/investigation-event";
import type { InvestigationId } from "../contracts/investigation-identity";
import type { InvestigationEventStore } from "../ports/investigation-event-store";

export class InMemoryInvestigationEventStore implements InvestigationEventStore {
  private readonly streams = new Map<InvestigationId, InvestigationEvent[]>();

  async append(id: InvestigationId, events: readonly InvestigationEvent[], expectedSequence: number): Promise<void> {
    const current = this.streams.get(id) ?? [];
    if (current.length !== expectedSequence)
      throw new Error(`Event sequence conflict: expected ${expectedSequence}, found ${current.length}.`);
    this.streams.set(id, [...current, ...events]);
  }

  async read(id: InvestigationId): Promise<readonly InvestigationEvent[]> {
    return [...(this.streams.get(id) ?? [])];
  }
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\kernel\testing\in-memory-investigation-repository.ts =====
import type { InvestigationId } from "../contracts/investigation-identity";
import type { InvestigationSnapshot } from "../contracts/investigation-snapshot";
import type { InvestigationRepository } from "../ports/investigation-repository";

export class InMemoryInvestigationRepository implements InvestigationRepository {
  private readonly values = new Map<InvestigationId, InvestigationSnapshot>();

  async load<TCanonical = unknown>(id: InvestigationId): Promise<InvestigationSnapshot<TCanonical> | undefined> {
    return this.values.get(id) as InvestigationSnapshot<TCanonical> | undefined;
  }

  async save<TCanonical = unknown>(snapshot: InvestigationSnapshot<TCanonical>, expectedRevision: number): Promise<void> {
    const current = this.values.get(snapshot.identity.investigationId);
    if (current && current.identity.revision !== expectedRevision)
      throw new Error(`Investigation revision conflict: expected ${expectedRevision}, found ${current.identity.revision}.`);
    this.values.set(snapshot.identity.investigationId, snapshot as InvestigationSnapshot);
  }
}

===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\persistence\handoff-store.ts =====
"use client";
import { LATTICE_HANDOFF_PREFIX, validateHandoff, type HandoffReadResult, type LatticeHandoff } from "../contracts/handoff";

export function writeHandoff(value: LatticeHandoff): HandoffReadResult {
  const valid = validateHandoff(value);
  if (!valid.ok) return valid;
  try {
    const key = `${LATTICE_HANDOFF_PREFIX}${value.handoffId}`;
    sessionStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(value));
    return readHandoff(value.handoffId);
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, code: "storage-unavailable", message: "The browser could not preserve the Lattice handoff." };
  }
}

export function readHandoff(id: string): HandoffReadResult {
  try {
    const raw = sessionStorage.getItem(`${LATTICE_HANDOFF_PREFIX}${id}`);
    if (!raw) return { ok: false, code: "not-found", message: "The Lattice handoff could not be found." };
    let parsed: unknown;
    try { parsed = JSON.parse(raw); } catch { return { ok: false, code: "serialization-failed", message: "The stored handoff could not be read." }; }
    const result = validateHandoff(parsed);
    if (!result.ok && result.code === "expired") sessionStorage.removeItem(`${LATTICE_HANDOFF_PREFIX}${id}`);
    return result;
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, code: "storage-unavailable", message: "Browser session storage is unavailable." };
  }
}




===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\persistence\investigation-store.ts =====
import type { InvestigationState } from "../state/investigation-reducer";
import type { InvestigationGraph, InvestigationSelection, RelationshipKind } from "../domain/model";
import { initialInvestigation } from "../state/investigation-reducer";
import { LATTICE_LAYOUT_REVISION } from "../layout/layout-contract";
import { LATTICE_PERSISTENCE_VERSION, migratePersistedInvestigation } from "./layout-migrations";

const VERSION = LATTICE_PERSISTENCE_VERSION;
const key = (id: string) => `lattice-next:investigation:${id}`;

export function clearInvestigation(id: string) {
  try { localStorage.removeItem(key(id)); return true; } catch { return false; }
}

export function saveInvestigation(id: string, state: InvestigationState) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(key(id), JSON.stringify({
      ...state,
      version: VERSION,
      layoutRevision: LATTICE_LAYOUT_REVISION,
      activeSources: [...state.activeSources],
      activeRelationships: [...state.activeRelationships],
      interaction: {
        ...state.interaction,
        expansion: {
          ...state.interaction.expansion,
          expanded: [...state.interaction.expansion.expanded],
        },
        animation: {
          enteringNodeIds: [...state.interaction.animation.enteringNodeIds],
          activeEdgeIds: [...state.interaction.animation.activeEdgeIds],
        },
      },
    }));
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

function validSelection(value: unknown, graph: InvestigationGraph): InvestigationSelection {
  const selection = value as InvestigationSelection | undefined;
  if (selection?.type === "entity" && graph.byId[selection.id]) return selection;
  if (selection?.type === "relationship" && graph.relationshipById[selection.id]) return selection;
  return { type: "none" };
}

export function loadInvestigation(id: string, graph: InvestigationGraph, sources: string[]): InvestigationState | null {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(key(id));
    if (!raw) return null;
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Record<string, unknown>;
    const migration = migratePersistedInvestigation(parsed);
    if (!migration.ok) return null;
    const value = migration.value;
    const fallback = initialInvestigation(graph.roots, sources);

    if (value.version !== VERSION || value.layoutRevision !== LATTICE_LAYOUT_REVISION || !value.interaction || typeof value.interaction !== "object") return null;
    const restored = value as unknown as Omit<InvestigationState, "activeSources" | "activeRelationships"> & {
      activeSources: string[];
      activeRelationships: RelationshipKind[];
      interaction: Omit<InvestigationState["interaction"], "expansion" | "animation"> & {
        expansion: { expanded: string[]; focusRoot: string | null };
        animation: { enteringNodeIds: string[]; activeEdgeIds: string[] };
      };
    };
    return {
      ...restored,
      revision: 3,
      activeSources: new Set(restored.activeSources.filter((source) => sources.includes(source))),
      activeRelationships: new Set(restored.activeRelationships),
      interaction: {
        ...restored.interaction,
        selection: validSelection(restored.interaction.selection, graph),
        pinnedPositions: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(restored.interaction.pinnedPositions ?? {}).filter(([nodeId]) => Boolean(graph.byId[nodeId]))),
        expansion: {
          focusRoot: restored.interaction.expansion.focusRoot && graph.byId[restored.interaction.expansion.focusRoot] ? restored.interaction.expansion.focusRoot : null,
          expanded: new Set(restored.interaction.expansion.expanded.filter((nodeId) => Boolean(graph.byId[nodeId]))),
        },
        animation: {
          enteringNodeIds: new Set(),
          activeEdgeIds: new Set(),
        },
        layoutMode: restored.interaction.layoutMode === "BILATERAL" ? "BILATERAL" : "DEFAULT",
      },
    };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\persistence\layout-migrations.ts =====
import { LATTICE_LAYOUT_REVISION } from "../layout/layout-contract";
export const LATTICE_PERSISTENCE_VERSION = 5 as const;
export type PersistedEnvelope = Record<string, unknown> & { version?: number; layoutRevision?: number; interaction?: Record<string, unknown> };
type Migration = (value: PersistedEnvelope) => PersistedEnvelope;
const migrate2to3: Migration = value => ({ ...value, version: 3 });
const migrate3to4: Migration = value => ({ ...value, version: 4, layoutRevision: LATTICE_LAYOUT_REVISION, interaction: { ...(value.interaction ?? {}), pinnedPositions: {} } });
const migrate4to5: Migration = value => ({ ...value, version: 5, layoutRevision: LATTICE_LAYOUT_REVISION, graphStructuralKey: typeof value.graphStructuralKey === "string" ? value.graphStructuralKey : "unknown" });
const migrations = new Map<number, Migration>([[2,migrate2to3],[3,migrate3to4],[4,migrate4to5]]);
export type MigrationResult = { ok: true; value: PersistedEnvelope; migratedFrom: number | null } | { ok: false; reason: "invalid"|"future-version"|"unsupported-version" };
export function migratePersistedInvestigation(input: unknown): MigrationResult {
  if (!input || typeof input !== "object" || Array.isArray(input)) return { ok:false, reason:"invalid" };
  let value={...(input as PersistedEnvelope)}; const from=typeof value.version==="number"?value.version:0;
  if (from>LATTICE_PERSISTENCE_VERSION) return {ok:false,reason:"future-version"};
  if (from<2) return {ok:false,reason:"unsupported-version"};
  while(value.version!==LATTICE_PERSISTENCE_VERSION){const migration=migrations.get(value.version!);if(!migration)return{ok:false,reason:"unsupported-version"};value=migration(value)}
  if(value.layoutRevision!==LATTICE_LAYOUT_REVISION)value={...value,layoutRevision:LATTICE_LAYOUT_REVISION,interaction:{...(value.interaction??{}),pinnedPositions:{}}};
  return {ok:true,value,migratedFrom:from===LATTICE_PERSISTENCE_VERSION?null:from};
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\state\investigation-reducer.ts =====
import type { XYPosition } from "@xyflow/react";
import type { LayoutOrientation, RendererViewport } from "../contracts/projection";
import type { RelationshipKind } from "../domain/model";
import { createInteractionState, type LatticeInteractionState } from "./lattice-interaction-state";

export type InvestigationState = {
  interaction: LatticeInteractionState;
  query: string;
  inspectorOpen: boolean;
  revision: 3;
  activeSources: Set<string>;
  activeRelationships: Set<RelationshipKind>;
  orientation: LayoutOrientation;
};

export type InvestigationAction =
  | { type: "select-entity"; id: string }
  | { type: "select-relationship"; id: string }
  | { type: "clear-transient" }
  | { type: "toggle"; id: string }
  | { type: "query"; value: string }
  | { type: "viewport"; value: RendererViewport }
  | { type: "pin-position"; id: string; position: XYPosition }
  | { type: "unpin-position"; id: string }
  | { type: "reset-layout" }
  | { type: "bilateral-reflow"; positions: Record<string, XYPosition> }
  | { type: "reset-selected" }
  | { type: "hover-entity"; id: string | null }
  | { type: "hover-relationship"; id: string | null }
  | { type: "focus"; id: string | null }
  | { type: "source"; value: string }
  | { type: "relationship"; value: RelationshipKind }
  | { type: "expand-many"; ids: string[] }
  | { type: "collapse-many"; ids: string[] }
  | { type: "orientation"; value: LayoutOrientation }
  | { type: "start-new-investigation" }
  | { type: "open-inspector" }
  | { type: "close-inspector" }
  | { type: "toggle-inspector" }
  | { type: "collapse-all" };

export function initialInvestigation(roots: string[], sources: string[]): InvestigationState {
  return {
    interaction: createInteractionState(roots),
    query: "",
    inspectorOpen: false,
    revision: 3,
    activeSources: new Set(sources),
    activeRelationships: new Set(["contains", "corresponds-to"]),
    orientation: "RIGHT",
  };
}

export function investigationReducer(state: InvestigationState, action: InvestigationAction): InvestigationState {
  switch (action.type) {
    case "start-new-investigation":
      return {
        ...state,
        query: "",
        inspectorOpen: false,
        interaction: {
          ...createInteractionState([]),
          viewport: { x: 0, y: 0, zoom: 1 },
        },
      };
    case "open-inspector": return { ...state, inspectorOpen: true };
    case "close-inspector": return { ...state, inspectorOpen: false };
    case "toggle-inspector": return { ...state, inspectorOpen: !state.inspectorOpen };
    case "collapse-all": return { ...state, interaction: { ...state.interaction, expansion: { expanded: new Set<string>(), focusRoot: null } } };
    case "select-entity":
      return { ...state, interaction: { ...state.interaction, selection: { type: "entity", id: action.id } } };
    case "select-relationship":
      return { ...state, interaction: { ...state.interaction, selection: { type: "relationship", id: action.id } } };
    case "clear-transient":
      return {
        ...state,
        interaction: {
          ...state.interaction,
          selection: { type: "none" },
          hover: { entityId: null, relationshipId: null },
          animation: { enteringNodeIds: new Set(), activeEdgeIds: new Set() },
        },
      };
    case "query":
      return { ...state, query: action.value };
    case "viewport":
      return { ...state, interaction: { ...state.interaction, viewport: action.value } };
    case "pin-position":
      return { ...state, interaction: { ...state.interaction, pinnedPositions: { ...state.interaction.pinnedPositions, [action.id]: action.position } } };
    case "unpin-position": {
      const pinnedPositions = { ...state.interaction.pinnedPositions };
      delete pinnedPositions[action.id];
      return { ...state, interaction: { ...state.interaction, pinnedPositions } };
    }
    case "reset-layout":
      return { ...state, interaction: { ...state.interaction, pinnedPositions: {}, layoutMode: "DEFAULT" } };
    case "bilateral-reflow":
      return { ...state, interaction: { ...state.interaction, pinnedPositions: action.positions, layoutMode: "BILATERAL" } };
    case "reset-selected": {
      const selection = state.interaction.selection;
      if (selection.type !== "entity") return state;
      const pinnedPositions = { ...state.interaction.pinnedPositions };
      delete pinnedPositions[selection.id];
      return { ...state, interaction: { ...state.interaction, pinnedPositions } };
    }
    case "hover-entity":
      return { ...state, interaction: { ...state.interaction, hover: { ...state.interaction.hover, entityId: action.id } } };
    case "hover-relationship":
      return { ...state, interaction: { ...state.interaction, hover: { ...state.interaction.hover, relationshipId: action.id } } };
    case "focus":
      return { ...state, interaction: { ...state.interaction, expansion: { ...state.interaction.expansion, focusRoot: action.id } } };
    case "orientation":
      return { ...state, orientation: action.value };
    case "source": {
      const activeSources = new Set(state.activeSources);
      if (activeSources.has(action.value)) activeSources.delete(action.value); else activeSources.add(action.value);
      return { ...state, activeSources };
    }
    case "relationship": {
      const activeRelationships = new Set(state.activeRelationships);
      if (activeRelationships.has(action.value)) activeRelationships.delete(action.value); else activeRelationships.add(action.value);
      return { ...state, activeRelationships };
    }
    case "expand-many": { const expanded=new Set(state.interaction.expansion.expanded); action.ids.forEach((id)=>expanded.add(id)); return {...state,interaction:{...state.interaction,expansion:{...state.interaction.expansion,expanded}}}; }
    case "collapse-many": { const expanded=new Set(state.interaction.expansion.expanded); action.ids.forEach((id)=>expanded.delete(id)); return {...state,interaction:{...state.interaction,expansion:{...state.interaction.expansion,expanded}}}; }
    case "toggle": {
      const expanded = new Set(state.interaction.expansion.expanded);
      if (expanded.has(action.id)) expanded.delete(action.id); else expanded.add(action.id);
      return { ...state, interaction: { ...state.interaction, expansion: { ...state.interaction.expansion, expanded } } };
    }
  }
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\state\lattice-interaction-state.ts =====
import type { XYPosition } from "@xyflow/react";
import type { InvestigationSelection } from "../domain/model";
import type { RendererViewport } from "../contracts/projection";
import type { BilateralLayoutMode } from "../layout/bilateral-layout-contract";

export type LatticeExpansionState = { expanded: Set<string>; focusRoot: string | null };
export type LatticeHoverState = { entityId: string | null; relationshipId: string | null };
export type LatticeAnimationState = { enteringNodeIds: Set<string>; activeEdgeIds: Set<string> };

/** UI-only interaction state. The canonical engineering graph is deliberately
 * absent from this type and remains immutable in the workspace/domain layer. */
export type LatticeInteractionState = {
  selection: InvestigationSelection;
  expansion: LatticeExpansionState;
  pinnedPositions: Record<string, XYPosition>;
  viewport: RendererViewport;
  hover: LatticeHoverState;
  animation: LatticeAnimationState;
  layoutMode: BilateralLayoutMode;
};

export function createInteractionState(roots: string[]): LatticeInteractionState {
  return {
    selection: roots[0] ? { type: "entity", id: roots[0] } : { type: "none" },
    expansion: { expanded: new Set(roots), focusRoot: null },
    pinnedPositions: {},
    viewport: { x: 0, y: 0, zoom: 1 },
    hover: { entityId: null, relationshipId: null },
    animation: { enteringNodeIds: new Set(), activeEdgeIds: new Set() },
    layoutMode: "DEFAULT",
  };
}

===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\tests\investigation-reducer.test.ts =====

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { initialInvestigation, investigationReducer } from "../state/investigation-reducer";

describe("investigation interaction reducer", () => {
  it("toggles a collapsed node open without clearing selection", () => {
    let state = initialInvestigation(["root"], ["unified", "teamcenter"]);
    state = investigationReducer(state, { type: "select-entity", id: "child" });
    state = investigationReducer(state, { type: "toggle", id: "child" });
    expect(state.interaction.expansion.expanded.has("child")).toBe(true);
    expect(state.interaction.selection).toEqual({ type: "entity", id: "child" });
  });

  it("toggles an initially expanded root closed", () => {
    let state = initialInvestigation(["root"], ["unified"]);
    expect(state.interaction.expansion.expanded.has("root")).toBe(true);
    state = investigationReducer(state, { type: "toggle", id: "root" });
    expect(state.interaction.expansion.expanded.has("root")).toBe(false);
  });

  it("collapses all requested descendants", () => {
    let state = initialInvestigation(["root"], ["unified"]);
    state = investigationReducer(state, { type: "expand-many", ids: ["root", "child", "leaf"] });
    state = investigationReducer(state, { type: "collapse-many", ids: ["root", "child", "leaf"] });
    expect([...state.interaction.expansion.expanded]).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("reset layout clears only pins and preserves expansion", () => {
    let state = initialInvestigation(["root"], ["unified"]);
    state = investigationReducer(state, { type: "toggle", id: "child" });
    state = investigationReducer(state, { type: "pin-position", id: "root", position: { x: 1, y: 2 } });
    state = investigationReducer(state, { type: "reset-layout" });
    expect(state.interaction.pinnedPositions).toEqual({});
    expect(state.interaction.expansion.expanded.has("child")).toBe(true);
    expect(state.interaction.expansion.expanded.has("root")).toBe(true);
  });


  it("starts a new investigation without clearing page-level source configuration", () => {
    let state = initialInvestigation(["root"], ["unified", "teamcenter"]);
    state = investigationReducer(state, { type: "select-entity", id: "child" });
    state = investigationReducer(state, { type: "pin-position", id: "child", position: { x: 12, y: 24 } });
    state = investigationReducer(state, { type: "query", value: "EYE HEAD" });
    const activeSources = state.activeSources;

    state = investigationReducer(state, { type: "start-new-investigation" });

    expect(state.query).toBe("");
    expect(state.inspectorOpen).toBe(false);
    expect(state.interaction.selection).toEqual({ type: "none" });
    expect(state.interaction.expansion.expanded.size).toBe(0);
    expect(state.interaction.expansion.focusRoot).toBeNull();
    expect(state.interaction.pinnedPositions).toEqual({});
    expect(state.interaction.hover).toEqual({ entityId: null, relationshipId: null });
    expect(state.activeSources).toBe(activeSources);
  });
});

===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\tests\layout-stability.test.ts =====
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  classifyChange,
  createSession,
  stabilizeLayout,
} from "../layout/layout-session";
import type { RelationshipProjection } from "../contracts/projection";
import type { UniversalLayoutResult } from "../layout/layout-contract";
const projection = (ids: string[], key = "k"): RelationshipProjection => ({
  structuralKey: key,
  nodes: ids.map((id, i) => ({
    id,
    label: id,
    subtitle: id,
    kind: i ? "component" : "identity",
    source: "x",
    level: i,
    selected: false,
    expanded: true,
    hasChildren: false,
    hiddenChildren: 0,
    relationshipCount: 0,
    childCount: 0,
  })),
  edges: [],
});
const result = (ids: string[]): UniversalLayoutResult => ({
  signal: 1,
  revision: 2,
  positions: Object.fromEntries(ids.map((id, i) => [id, { x: i * 100, y: 0 }])),
  dimensions: Object.fromEntries(
    ids.map((id) => [id, { width: 50, height: 30 }]),
  ),
  ports: {},
  routes: {},
  branchBounds: ids[1]
    ? { [ids[1]]: { x: 100, y: 0, width: 50, height: 30 } }
    : {},
  branches: ids[1]
    ? [
        {
          id: ids[1],
          rootId: ids[1],
          nodeIds: ids.slice(1),
          edgeIds: [],
          direction: "RIGHT",
          angle: 0,
          bounds: { x: 100, y: 0, width: 50, height: 30 },
        },
      ]
    : [],
  radialConnections: {},
  diagnostics: {
    strategy: "sector-layered-v2",
    sourceCount: 1,
    rings: 1,
    iterations: 1,
    collisionsResolved: 0,
    elapsedMs: 1,
    overlapCount: 0,
    invalidCoordinateCount: 0,
    fallbackBranches: 0,
    nodeOverlapCount: 0,
    branchOverlapCount: 0,
    subjectIntrusionCount: 0,
    invalidRouteCount: 0,
  },
});
describe("incremental stability", () => {
  it("classifies expansion and collapse", () => {
    const before = createSession(result(["s", "a"]), projection(["s", "a"]), 1);
    expect(classifyChange(before, projection(["s", "a", "b"]), {})).toBe(
      "expand",
    );
    expect(classifyChange(before, projection(["s"]), {})).toBe("collapse");
  });
  it("preserves unchanged branch anchors", () => {
    const old = result(["s", "a"]);
    old.positions.a = { x: 500, y: 200 };
    old.branchBounds.a = { x: 500, y: 200, width: 50, height: 30 };
    const session = createSession(old, projection(["s", "a"]), 1);
    const next = result(["s", "a"]);
    const stable = stabilizeLayout(next, session, "filter");
    expect(stable.positions.a).toEqual({ x: 500, y: 200 });
  });
  it("tracks generations", () => {
    expect(
      createSession(result(["s"]), projection(["s"]), 42).layoutGeneration,
    ).toBe(42);
  });
});

===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\tests\radial-connection-geometry.test.ts =====
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  radialConnectionGeometry,
  rectangleBoundaryAttachment,
} from "../layout/radial-connection-geometry";

const center = { x: 100, y: 100 };
const size = { width: 200, height: 100 };

function liesOnBoundary(point: { x: number; y: number }) {
  const onVertical =
    Math.abs(Math.abs(point.x - center.x) - size.width / 2) < 1e-8;
  const onHorizontal =
    Math.abs(Math.abs(point.y - center.y) - size.height / 2) < 1e-8;
  return onVertical || onHorizontal;
}

describe("radial rectangle connection geometry", () => {
  it.each([
    [0, "EAST", "WEST"],
    [Math.PI / 2, "SOUTH", "NORTH"],
    [Math.PI, "WEST", "EAST"],
    [-Math.PI / 2, "NORTH", "SOUTH"],
    [Math.PI / 4, "EAST", "WEST"],
    [(3 * Math.PI) / 4, "SOUTH", "NORTH"],
    [(-3 * Math.PI) / 4, "NORTH", "SOUTH"],
    [-Math.PI / 4, "EAST", "WEST"],
  ])(
    "allocates angle %s to opposing rectangle sides",
    (angle, sourceSide, targetSide) => {
      const result = radialConnectionGeometry({
        branchId: `branch-${angle}`,
        angle,
        sourceCenter: center,
        sourceSize: size,
        targetCenter: center,
        targetSize: size,
      });
      expect(result.source.side).toBe(sourceSide);
      expect(result.target.side).toBe(targetSide);
      expect(liesOnBoundary(result.source.position)).toBe(true);
      expect(liesOnBoundary(result.target.position)).toBe(true);
      expect(Number.isFinite(result.source.position.x)).toBe(true);
      expect(Number.isFinite(result.target.position.y)).toBe(true);
    },
  );

  it("keeps same-side branches on unique deterministic handles", () => {
    const first = radialConnectionGeometry({
      branchId: "a",
      angle: -0.35,
      sourceCenter: center,
      sourceSize: size,
      targetCenter: center,
      targetSize: size,
    });
    const second = radialConnectionGeometry({
      branchId: "b",
      angle: 0.35,
      sourceCenter: center,
      sourceSize: size,
      targetCenter: center,
      targetSize: size,
    });
    expect(first.source.side).toBe("EAST");
    expect(second.source.side).toBe("EAST");
    expect(first.source.id).toBe("radial-source:a");
    expect(second.source.id).toBe("radial-source:b");
    expect(first.source.offsetPercent).not.toBe(second.source.offsetPercent);
    expect(
      radialConnectionGeometry({
        branchId: "a",
        angle: -0.35,
        sourceCenter: center,
        sourceSize: size,
        targetCenter: center,
        targetSize: size,
      }),
    ).toEqual(first);
  });

  it("safely handles a zero direction component and clamps away from corners", () => {
    const result = rectangleBoundaryAttachment({
      id: "radial-source:zero",
      branchId: "zero",
      center,
      size,
      direction: { x: 0, y: -1 },
    });
    expect(result.side).toBe("NORTH");
    expect(result.position).toEqual({ x: 100, y: 50 });
    expect(result.offsetPercent).toBe(50);
    expect(result.offsetPercent).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(8);
    expect(result.offsetPercent).toBeLessThanOrEqual(92);
  });

  it("covers all four sides with eight deterministic branches", () => {
    const results = Array.from({ length: 8 }, (_, index) =>
      radialConnectionGeometry({
        branchId: `branch-${index}`,
        angle: -Math.PI / 2 + (index * Math.PI) / 4,
        sourceCenter: center,
        sourceSize: size,
        targetCenter: center,
        targetSize: size,
      }),
    );
    expect(new Set(results.map((result) => result.source.side))).toEqual(
      new Set(["NORTH", "EAST", "SOUTH", "WEST"]),
    );
    expect(new Set(results.map((result) => result.source.id)).size).toBe(8);
  });
});


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\tests\radial-layout.test.ts =====
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { radialChildPositions } from "../infrastructure/hybrid-layout-planner";
const angle=(p:{x:number;y:number})=>Math.atan2(p.y,p.x);
const separation=(a:number,b:number)=>{const raw=Math.abs(a-b)%(Math.PI*2);return Math.min(raw,Math.PI*2-raw)};
describe("360/n radial geometry",()=>{
 it("places two children exactly 180 degrees apart",()=>{const p=radialChildPositions({x:0,y:0},["b","a"],300,0);expect(separation(angle(p.a),angle(p.b))).toBeCloseTo(Math.PI,8);});
 it("places three children 120 degrees apart",()=>{const p=radialChildPositions({x:0,y:0},["c","a","b"],300,0);const values=Object.values(p).map(angle).sort((a,b)=>a-b);const gaps=[values[1]-values[0],values[2]-values[1],Math.PI*2-(values[2]-values[0])];gaps.forEach(gap=>expect(gap).toBeCloseTo((Math.PI*2)/3,8));});
 it("is deterministic regardless of input order",()=>{expect(radialChildPositions({x:10,y:20},["z","a"],200,0)).toEqual(radialChildPositions({x:10,y:20},["a","z"],200,0));});
});


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\tests\radial-route-adapter.test.ts =====
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { applyRadialFirstHopRoutes } from "../layout/radial-route-adapter";
import type { RadialLayoutConnection } from "../layout/layout-contract";

const connection: RadialLayoutConnection = {
  branchId: "branch-a",
  angle: Math.PI,
  sourceNodeId: "subject",
  targetNodeId: "branch-a",
  edgeId: "edge-a",
  source: { id: "radial-source:branch-a", nodeId: "subject", branchId: "branch-a", side: "WEST", offsetPercent: 50, position: { x: 10, y: 20 } },
  target: { id: "radial-target:branch-a", nodeId: "branch-a", branchId: "branch-a", side: "EAST", offsetPercent: 50, position: { x: -100, y: 20 } },
};

describe("radial first-hop route adaptation", () => {
  it("replaces a synthetic hub route with exact radial endpoints", () => {
    const result = applyRadialFirstHopRoutes({
      "edge-a": { edgeId: "edge-a", start: { x: 100, y: 20 }, bends: [{ x: 180, y: 20 }], end: { x: -90, y: 20 }, family: "structure" },
    }, { "edge-a": connection });
    expect(result["edge-a"].start).toEqual(connection.source.position);
    expect(result["edge-a"].end).toEqual(connection.target.position);
    expect(result["edge-a"].bends).toEqual([]);
    expect(result["edge-a"].label).toEqual({ x: -45, y: 20 });
  });

  it("preserves non-radial routes and is deterministic", () => {
    const routes = { other: { edgeId: "other", start: { x: 0, y: 0 }, bends: [{ x: 1, y: 1 }], end: { x: 2, y: 2 } } };
    const first = applyRadialFirstHopRoutes(routes, { "edge-a": connection });
    const second = applyRadialFirstHopRoutes(routes, { "edge-a": connection });
    expect(first.other).toEqual(routes.other);
    expect(first).toEqual(second);
  });
});

===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\tests\security-hardening.test.ts =====
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { resolve } from "node:path";

const root = (path: string) => resolve(process.cwd(), "../..", path);

describe("AUD-001 secret containment", () => {
  it("loads the ignored root .env automatically and contains no embedded key", () => {
    const script = readFileSync(root("configit_extractor/api call.ps1"), "utf8");
    expect(script).not.toMatch(/Authorization\s*=\s*["']ApiKey\s+[A-Za-z0-9_\-=]{20,}/);
    expect(script).toContain('Join-Path $platformRoot ".env"');
    expect(script).toContain("Import-RootDotEnv");
    expect(script).toContain("CONFIGIT_API_KEY");
    expect(script).toContain("CONFIGIT_PRODUCT_ID");
  });

  it("ships a blank commit-safe .env.example rather than a usable credential", () => {
    const example = readFileSync(root(".env.example"), "utf8");
    expect(example).toMatch(/^CONFIGIT_API_KEY=\s*$/m);
    expect(example).toMatch(/^CONFIGIT_PRODUCT_ID=\s*$/m);
    expect(example).not.toMatch(/^CONFIGIT_API_KEY=.{12,}$/m);
  });

  it("ignores local environment files while allowing safe examples", () => {
    const ignore = readFileSync(root(".gitignore"), "utf8");
    expect(ignore).toMatch(/^\.env$/m);
    expect(ignore).toMatch(/^\.env\.\*$/m);
    expect(ignore).toMatch(/^!\.env\.example$/m);
    expect(ignore).toMatch(/^!\*\*\/\.env\.example$/m);
  });
});

===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\tests\universal-layout.test.ts =====
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { RelationshipProjection } from "../contracts/projection";
import { decomposeProjection } from "../layout/branch-decomposition";
import { buildElkBranchGraph } from "../layout/elk-branch-builder";
import { boundsOverlap, sectorAngles, usableViewport } from "../layout/sector-orchestrator";
import { hybridLayout } from "../infrastructure/hybrid-layout-planner";

const projection = (branches: number, depth = 3, width = 2): RelationshipProjection => {
  const nodes: RelationshipProjection["nodes"] = [{ id:"subject",label:"Subject",subtitle:"Universal",kind:"identity",source:"unified",level:0,selected:false,expanded:true,hasChildren:true,hiddenChildren:0,relationshipCount:branches,childCount:branches }];
  const edges: RelationshipProjection["edges"] = [];
  for (let branch=0; branch<branches; branch++) {
    let parents=["subject"];
    for (let level=1; level<=depth; level++) {
      const next:string[]=[];
      for (const parent of parents) for (let child=0; child<width; child++) {
        const id=`b${branch}-l${level}-${parent}-${child}`; next.push(id);
        nodes.push({id,label:id,subtitle:"Generated",kind:level===depth?"component":"assembly",source:`source-${branch}`,level,selected:false,expanded:true,hasChildren:level<depth,hiddenChildren:0,relationshipCount:1,childCount:level<depth?width:0});
        edges.push({id:`e:${parent}:${id}`,source:parent,target:id,kind:parent==="subject"?"represented-by":"contains",selected:false});
      }
      parents=next;
    }
  }
  return {nodes,edges,structuralKey:`${branches}:${depth}:${width}`};
};

describe("universal sector-layered architecture",()=>{
  it("decomposes arbitrary topology into primary branches",()=>{const graph=projection(5,2,2);const result=decomposeProjection(graph)!;expect(result.subjectId).toBe("subject");expect(result.branches).toHaveLength(10);expect(new Set(result.branches.flatMap(b=>b.nodeIds)).size).toBe(graph.nodes.length-1);});
  it("builds deterministic ELK graphs with explicit ordered ports",()=>{const graph=projection(2,2,1);const branch=decomposeProjection(graph)!.branches[0];const a=buildElkBranchGraph(branch,graph,"RIGHT");const b=buildElkBranchGraph(branch,graph,"RIGHT");expect(a).toEqual(b);expect(a.children.every(n=>n.ports.length===2)).toBe(true);expect(a.layoutOptions["elk.edgeRouting"]).toBe("ORTHOGONAL");});
  it("allocates exact 360/n sectors for any branch count",()=>{const values=sectorAngles(["d","a","c","b"]);const gaps=values.map((v,i)=>((values[(i+1)%values.length].angle-v.angle+Math.PI*2)%(Math.PI*2)));expect(gaps.every(g=>Math.abs(g-Math.PI/2)<1e-9)).toBe(true);});
  it("accounts for inspector, toolbar and gutters",()=>{expect(usableViewport({width:1600,height:1000,inspectorWidth:400,toolbarHeight:100,gutter:50})).toMatchObject({width:1100,height:800,center:{x:550,y:400}});});
  it("composes a complete deterministic non-overlapping investigation",async()=>{const graph=projection(4,3,2);const a=await hybridLayout(graph,{},1,{width:1800,height:1200,inspectorWidth:360});const b=await hybridLayout(graph,{},1,{width:1800,height:1200,inspectorWidth:360});expect(a.positions).toEqual(b.positions);expect(Object.keys(a.positions)).toHaveLength(graph.nodes.length);expect(a.diagnostics.invalidCoordinateCount).toBe(0);for(let i=0;i<a.branches.length;i++)for(let j=i+1;j<a.branches.length;j++)expect(boundsOverlap(a.branches[i].bounds,a.branches[j].bounds,0)).toBe(false);});
});


describe("production layout invariants",()=>{
  it("returns valid ELK ports and finite routed structural edges",async()=>{const graph=projection(3,3,2);const result=await hybridLayout(graph,{},9,{width:1700,height:1050});expect(Object.keys(result.ports).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);expect(Object.keys(result.routes).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);expect(result.diagnostics.invalidRouteCount).toBe(0);for(const port of Object.values(result.ports)){expect(result.positions[port.nodeId]).toBeDefined();expect(Number.isFinite(port.position.x)&&Number.isFinite(port.position.y)).toBe(true)}});
  it("keeps branches outside the subject exclusion zone",async()=>{const result=await hybridLayout(projection(6,2,3),{},4,{width:1900,height:1200});expect(result.diagnostics.subjectIntrusionCount).toBe(0);expect(result.diagnostics.branchOverlapCount).toBe(0);});
  it("handles zero and one branch without invalid geometry",async()=>{for(const count of [0,1]){const result=await hybridLayout(projection(count,2,1),{},count);expect(result.diagnostics.invalidCoordinateCount).toBe(0);expect(Object.values(result.positions).every(point=>Number.isFinite(point.x)&&Number.isFinite(point.y))).toBe(true)}});
  it("honors a valid pin without losing graph coordinates",async()=>{const graph=projection(2,2,1);const target=graph.nodes[1].id;const result=await hybridLayout(graph,{[target]:{x:91,y:37}},7);expect(result.positions[target]).toEqual({x:91,y:37});expect(Object.keys(result.positions)).toHaveLength(graph.nodes.length);});
});
